EXECUTIVE READING

先给结论，再进入源码

核心机制

它不像一个简单 while 循环，更像一间控制室：用户输入、工具结果、文件变化、后台任务、模型切换都从不同通道进来，由同一个会话 Actor 排队处理。

证据规模

17 条 finding / 16 处引用

主要边界

已定位 sampler 与 Responses/Chat Completions 流；报告正文再展开协议差异。

适用建设

它不像一个简单 while 循环，更像一间控制室：用户输入、工具结果、文件变化、后台任务、模型切换都从不同通道进来，由同一个会话 Actor 排队处理。

值得借鉴

  • 它不像一个简单 while 循环，更像一间控制室：用户输入、工具结果、文件变化、后台任务、模型切换都从不同通道进来，由同一个会话 Actor 排队处理。
  • 能并行的尽量并行，但两个工具若同时写同一个文件会排队，避免互相覆盖。
  • 模型偶尔把两个 JSON 粘在一起，Grok Build 会先抢救，不是一看到格式错就整轮失败。

需要警惕

  • 已定位 sampler 与 Responses/Chat Completions 流；报告正文再展开协议差异。
  • 关键并发、安全、恢复测试已定位；完整测试规模待汇总。
  • 当前源码证据仍需部署级验证

阅读方式

  • 先看固定提交，再看调用链
  • 事实、推断、风险分栏
  • 每条 finding 可回到源码
00 · METHOD

研究口径：不是 README 摘要

README POLICY

README 只定位入口和产品命名；实现结论必须回到运行时代码、契约或测试。

FACT POLICY

L1 运行代码优先，L2 类型/事件契约补足，L3/L4 交叉验证，L5 推断单独标记。

tool.apply_patch

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · crates/codegen/xai-grok-tools/src/implementations/codex/apply_patch/tool.rs:295
源码 ↗

ctx: xai_tool_runtime::ToolCallContext,

tool.apply_patch→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  290              tool_scope: Some(xai_tool_protocol::ToolScope::Write),
  291              ..Default::default()
  292          }
  293      }
  294
  295      #[tracing::instrument(name = "tool.apply_patch", skip_all)]
  296      async fn run(
  297          &self,
  298          ctx: xai_tool_runtime::ToolCallContext,
  299          input: ApplyPatchInput,
  300      ) -> Result<ApplyPatchOutput, xai_tool_runtime::ToolError> {
  301          use crate::types::tool_metadata::shared_resources;
  302          let resources = shared_resources(&ctx)?;
  303
  304          let (cwd, fs, notification_handle);
  305          {
  306              cwd = crate::types::tool_metadata::resolve_cwd(&ctx, &resources).await?;
  307              let res = resources.lock().await;
  308              fs = res.require::<FileSystem>()?.0.clone();
  309              notification_handle = res.require::<NotificationHandle>()?.0.clone();
  310          }
  311          let tool_call_id = ctx.call_id.as_str().to_owned();
  312
  313          // ── Phase 1: Parse ───────────────────────────────────────
  314          let parsed = match parser::parse_patch(&input.patch) {
  315              Ok(p) => p,
  316              Err(e) => {
  317                  let msg = match &e {
  318                      ParseError::InvalidPatchError(m) => format!("Invalid patch: {m}"),
  319                      ParseError::InvalidHunkError {
  320                          message,
  321                          line_number,
  322                      } => format!("Invalid patch hunk on line {line_number}: {message}"),
  323                  };
  324                  return Ok(ApplyPatchOutput::ParseError(msg));
  325              }
  326          };
  327
  328          if parsed.hunks.is_empty() {
  329              return Ok(ApplyPatchOutput::EmptyPatch(
  330                  "No files were modified.".to_string(),
  331              ));
  332          }
  333
  334          // ── Phase 2: Compute all changes in memory (no writes yet) ───
  335          let changes = match compute_all_changes(&cwd, &fs, &parsed.hunks).await {
  336              Ok(c) => c,
  337              Err(msg) => return Ok(ApplyPatchOutput::ApplicationError(msg)),
  338          };
  339
  340          // ── Phase 3: Apply all changes (write to filesystem) ─────
  341          let mut file_results = Vec::new();
  342
  343          for change in &changes {
  344              match change {
  345                  FileChange::Add { path, content } => {
  346                      // Create parent directories if needed.
  347                      ensure_parent_dirs(path).await?;
  348                      fs.write_file(path, content.as_bytes()).await.map_err(|e| {
  349                          xai_tool_runtime::ToolError::execution(
  350                              xai_tool_protocol::ToolId::new("apply_patch").expect("valid"),
  351                              e.to_string(),
  352                          )
  353                      })?;
  354
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

tool.codex_grep_files→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  178              tool_scope: Some(xai_tool_protocol::ToolScope::Read),
  179              ..Default::default()
  180          }
  181      }
  182
  183      #[tracing::instrument(name = "tool.codex_grep_files", skip_all)]
  184      async fn run(
  185          &self,
  186          ctx: xai_tool_runtime::ToolCallContext,
  187          input: CodexGrepFilesInput,
  188      ) -> Result<CodexGrepFilesOutput, xai_tool_runtime::ToolError> {
  189          use crate::types::tool_metadata::shared_resources;
  190          let resources = shared_resources(&ctx)?;
  191
  192          let cwd = crate::types::tool_metadata::resolve_cwd(&ctx, &resources).await?;
  193
  194          // Validation (exact codex rules)
  195          let pattern = input.pattern.trim().to_string();
  196          if pattern.is_empty() {
  197              return Ok(CodexGrepFilesOutput::Error(
  198                  "pattern must not be empty".to_string(),
  199              ));
  200          }
  201          if input.limit == 0 {
  202              return Ok(CodexGrepFilesOutput::Error(
  203                  "limit must be greater than zero".to_string(),
  204              ));
  205          }
  206
  207          let limit = input.limit.min(MAX_LIMIT);
  208
  209          // Resolve search path
  210          let search_path = match &input.path {
  211              Some(p) if !p.is_empty() => {
  212                  let p = PathBuf::from(p);
  213                  if p.is_absolute() { p } else { cwd.join(p) }
  214              }
  215              _ => cwd.clone(),
  216          };
  217
  218          // Verify path exists
  219          if let Err(err) = tokio::fs::metadata(&search_path).await {
  220              return Ok(CodexGrepFilesOutput::Error(format!(
  221                  "unable to access `{}`: {err}",
  222                  search_path.display()
  223              )));
  224          }
  225
  226          // Clean up include glob
  227          let include = input.include.as_deref().map(str::trim).and_then(|v| {
  228              if v.is_empty() {
  229                  None
  230              } else {
  231                  Some(v.to_string())
  232              }
  233          });
  234
  235          // Run rg
  236          let results = run_rg_search(&pattern, include.as_deref(), &search_path, limit, &cwd).await;
  237
  238          match results {
  239              Ok(files) if files.is_empty() => Ok(CodexGrepFilesOutput::NoMatches(
  240                  "No matches found.".to_string(),
  241              )),
  242              Ok(files) => {

tool.codex_list_dir

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · crates/codegen/xai-grok-tools/src/implementations/codex/list_dir/tool.rs:303
源码 ↗

[tracing::instrument(

tool.codex_list_dir→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  298              ..Default::default()
  299          }
  300      }
  301
  302      #[tracing::instrument(
  303          name = "tool.codex_list_dir",
  304          skip_all,
  305          fields(path = %input.dir_path)
  306      )]
  307      async fn run(
  308          &self,
  309          _ctx: xai_tool_runtime::ToolCallContext,
  310          input: CodexListDirInput,
  311      ) -> Result<ListDirOutput, xai_tool_runtime::ToolError> {
  312          let CodexListDirInput {
  313              dir_path,
  314              offset,
  315              limit,
  316              depth,
  317          } = input;
  318
  319          if offset == 0 {
  320              return Ok(ListDirOutput::Error(
  321                  "offset must be a 1-indexed entry number".to_string(),
  322              ));
  323          }
  324          if limit == 0 {
  325              return Ok(ListDirOutput::Error(
  326                  "limit must be greater than zero".to_string(),
  327              ));
  328          }
  329          if depth == 0 {
  330              return Ok(ListDirOutput::Error(
  331                  "depth must be greater than zero".to_string(),
  332              ));
  333          }
  334          let path = PathBuf::from(&dir_path);
  335          if !path.is_absolute() {
  336              return Ok(ListDirOutput::Error(
  337                  "dir_path must be an absolute path".to_string(),
  338              ));
  339          }
  340
  341          let entries = match list_dir_slice(&path, offset, limit, depth).await {
  342              Ok(entries) => entries,
  343              Err(msg) => return Ok(ListDirOutput::Error(msg)),
  344          };
  345
  346          let mut output = Vec::with_capacity(entries.len() + 1);
  347          output.push(format!("Absolute path: {}", path.display()));
  348          output.extend(entries);
  349          let content = output.join("\n");
  350
  351          Ok(ListDirOutput::Content(ListDirContent {
  352              content,
  353              absolute_root_path: path,
  354          }))
  355      }
  356  }

tool.codex_read_file

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · crates/codegen/xai-grok-tools/src/implementations/codex/read_file/tool.rs:174
源码 ↗

ctx: xai_tool_runtime::ToolCallContext,

tool.codex_read_file→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  169              tool_scope: Some(xai_tool_protocol::ToolScope::Read),
  170              ..Default::default()
  171          }
  172      }
  173
  174      #[tracing::instrument(name = "tool.codex_read_file", skip_all)]
  175      async fn run(
  176          &self,
  177          ctx: xai_tool_runtime::ToolCallContext,
  178          input: CodexReadFileInput,
  179      ) -> Result<ReadFileOutput, xai_tool_runtime::ToolError> {
  180          use crate::types::tool_metadata::shared_resources;
  181          let resources = shared_resources(&ctx)?;
  182
  183          // 1. Validate. Codex raises here, but we surface these as a structured
  184          // `FileReadError` (a model-facing error) instead of a hard `Err`, so
  185          // otherwise-benign validation failures (empty/short files, relative
  186          // paths) do not surface as tool-execution failures.
  187          // `FileReadError` rides the structured-output path and maps cleanly to
  188          // `ReadFileErrorTypes::FILE_READ_ERROR`.
  189          if input.offset == 0 {
  190              return Ok(ReadFileOutput::FileReadError(
  191                  "offset must be a 1-indexed line number".to_string(),
  192              ));
  193          }
  194          if input.limit == 0 {
  195              return Ok(ReadFileOutput::FileReadError(
  196                  "limit must be greater than zero".to_string(),
  197              ));
  198          }
  199          let path = PathBuf::from(&input.file_path);
  200          if !path.is_absolute() {
  201              return Ok(ReadFileOutput::FileReadError(
  202                  "file_path must be an absolute path".to_string(),
  203              ));
  204          }
  205
  206          // 2. Read file via AsyncFileSystem.
  207          let fs;
  208          {
  209              fs = resources.lock().await.require::<FileSystem>()?.0.clone();
  210          }
  211          let file_bytes = match fs.read_file(&path).await {
  212              Ok(bytes) => bytes,
  213              Err(e) => {
  214                  return Ok(ReadFileOutput::FileReadError(format!(
  215                      "Failed to read file: {}, {e}",
  216                      path.display()
  217                  )));
  218              }
  219          };
  220
  221          // 3. Branch on mode. Out-of-range / empty-file reads return a structured
  222          // `FileReadError` (see note above) instead of a hard `Err`.
  223          let collected = match input.mode {
  224              ReadMode::Slice => match slice::read_slice(&file_bytes, input.offset, input.limit) {
  225                  Ok(lines) => lines,
  226                  Err(e) => return Ok(ReadFileOutput::FileReadError(e)),
  227              },
  228              ReadMode::Indentation => {
  229                  let args = input.indentation.unwrap_or_default();
  230                  let options = indentation::IndentationOptions {
  231                      anchor_line: args.anchor_line,
  232                      max_levels: args.max_levels,
  233                      include_siblings: args.include_siblings,
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

  316  impl AgentArgs {
  317      /// Canonicalized `--plugin-dir` paths, warning to stderr and skipping
  318      /// anything that isn't an existing directory (stderr is safe: JSON-RPC
  319      /// rides stdout).
  320      pub fn canonical_plugin_dirs(&self) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
  321          self.plugin_dirs
  322              .iter()
  323              .filter_map(|p| match dunce::canonicalize(p) {
  324                  Ok(canonical) if canonical.is_dir() => Some(canonical),
  325                  Ok(_) => {
  326                      eprintln!(
  327                          "grok: --plugin-dir {}: not a directory; skipping",
  328                          p.display()
  329                      );
  330                      None
  331                  }
  332                  Err(e) => {
  333                      eprintln!("grok: --plugin-dir {}: {e}; skipping", p.display());
  334                      None
  335                  }
  336              })
  337              .collect()
  338      }
  339  }

CLI 子命令 · clap Subcommand variant · crates/codegen/ptyctl-cli/src/cli.rs:185
源码 ↗

/ List registered sessions

list→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  180          #[arg(short, long, default_value = "10")]
  181          timeout: u64,
  182      },
  183
  184      /// List registered sessions
  185      List {
  186          /// Output as JSON
  187          #[arg(short, long)]
  188          json: bool,
  189      },
  190  }
  191
  192  /// Target session — exactly one of host, port, or name must be provided.
  193  #[derive(clap::Args)]
  194  #[group(required = true, multiple = false)]
  195  pub struct Target {
  196      /// Remote host address (e.g. 127.0.0.1:8080)
  197      #[arg(short = 'H', long)]
  198      pub host: Option<String>,
  199
  200      /// Local server port
  201      #[arg(short, long)]
  202      pub port: Option<u16>,
  203
  204      /// Session name (from registry)
  205      #[arg(short, long)]
  206      pub name: Option<String>,
  207  }
  208
  209  impl Target {
  210      /// Resolve target to a base URL.
  211      pub fn to_url(&self) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
  212          if let Some(ref h) = self.host {
  213              if h.starts_with("http") {
  214                  return Ok(h.clone());
  215              }
  216              return Ok(format!("http://{h}"));
  217          }
  218          if let Some(p) = self.port {
  219              return Ok(format!("http://127.0.0.1:{p}"));
  220          }
  221          if let Some(ref n) = self.name {
  222              let info = crate::registry::lookup_session(n)?;
  223              return Ok(format!("http://127.0.0.1:{}", info.port));
  224          }
  225          anyhow::bail!("no target specified")
  226      }
  227  }
  228

   74  sequences from its output, and writes them to your local system clipboard. This
   75  makes copy work when the program runs somewhere that cannot reach your
   76  clipboard (containers, SSH) and your terminal does not handle OSC 52 itself
   77  (for example Apple Terminal). The wrapped command's terminal is also kept in
   78  sync with your window size.
   79
   80  Examples:
   81    grok wrap docker exec -it my-container bash
   82    grok wrap kubectl exec -it my-pod -- bash
   83
   84  See ~/.grok/README.md for more information.

  326                      eprintln!(
  327                          "grok: --plugin-dir {}: not a directory; skipping",
  328                          p.display()
  329                      );
  330                      None
  331                  }
  332                  Err(e) => {
  333                      eprintln!("grok: --plugin-dir {}: {e}; skipping", p.display());

  317      #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]

展开逐行源码证据
  111                  elapsed,
  112              );
  113          }
  114          #[cfg(unix)]
  115          {
  116              let mut c = tokio::process::Command::new("sh");
  117              c.arg("-c").arg(command_str.as_ref());
  118              c
  119          }
  120          #[cfg(not(unix))]
  121          {
  122              let inv = xai_grok_config::shell::shell_command_argv(&command_str);
  123              let mut c = tokio::process::Command::new(&inv.program);
  124              c.args(&inv.args).envs(inv.env);
  125              c
  126          }
  127      } else {
  128          let command_path = if command.is_absolute() {
  129              command.clone()
  130          } else {
  131              spec.source_dir.join(command)
  132          };
  133          if !command_path.exists() {
  134              let elapsed = start.elapsed();
  135              return (
  136                  HookRunnerResult::Failed(format!("command not found: {}", command_path.display())),
  137                  elapsed,
  138              );
  139          }
  140          tokio::process::Command::new(command_path)
  141      };
  142
  143      // Detach from the controlling terminal so children (e.g. GPG pinentry)
  144      // can't open /dev/tty and corrupt the TUI display.
  145      xai_grok_tools::util::detach_command(&mut cmd);
  146
  147      // Spawn the child process.
  148      //
  149      // SECURITY: env-var precedence at spawn time. `Command::envs(&map)` runs
  150      // AFTER any preceding `.env(...)` calls and silently overrides them, so
  151      // the order matters: we MUST apply user/plugin `extra_env` FIRST and
  152      // the runner-injected vars LAST. Otherwise a user JSON hook (or a
  153      // plugin) can spoof `GROK_HOOK_EVENT`, `GROK_HOOK_NAME`, `GROK_SESSION_ID`,
  154      // `GROK_WORKSPACE_ROOT`, or `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR`, which are the
  155      // identity/event signals a hook script consumes for policy and audit.
  156      // See the `runner_injected_vars_override_extra_env_at_spawn`
  157      // regression test in `tests/integration.rs` and the rustdoc on
  158      // `HookSpec::extra_env`.
  159      #[allow(clippy::disallowed_methods)] // enrolled in the session scope below
  160      let mut child = match cmd
  161          .stdin(std::process::Stdio::piped())
  162          .stdout(std::process::Stdio::piped())
  163          .stderr(std::process::Stdio::piped())
  164          .current_dir(ctx.workspace_root)
  165          // 1. user/plugin extra_env first (lowest precedence).
  166          .envs(&spec.extra_env)
  167          // 2. runner-injected vars last (highest precedence, always win).
  168          .env("GROK_HOOK_EVENT", envelope.hook_event_name.to_string())
  169          .env("GROK_HOOK_NAME", &spec.name)
  170          .env("GROK_SESSION_ID", ctx.session_id)
  171          .env("GROK_WORKSPACE_ROOT", ctx.workspace_root)
  172          // Compatibility alias for external hooks that read this env name.
  173          // Same value as `GROK_WORKSPACE_ROOT`; native `.grok` hooks should use
  174          // `GROK_WORKSPACE_ROOT`.
  175          .env("CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR", ctx.workspace_root)

  343      /// Run the agent over stdio
  344      Stdio,
  345      /// Run the agent headlessly over the Grok WebSocket relay
  346      Headless(HeadlessArgs),
  347      /// Run the agent as a WebSocket server
  348      Serve(ServeArgs),
  349      /// Run as the shared leader process for other clients
  350      Leader(LeaderArgs),
  351  }
  352  /// WebSocket URL override arguments, used by headless / leader / serve modes.
  353  #[derive(Debug, clap::Args, Clone, Default)]
  354  pub struct HeadlessArgs {
  355      #[arg(long = "grok-ws-origin")]
  356      pub grok_ws_origin: Option<String>,
  357      #[arg(long = "grok-ws-url")]
  358      pub grok_ws_url: Option<String>,
  359  }
  360  /// Arguments for the `agent serve` subcommand.
  361  #[derive(Debug, clap::Args, Clone)]
  362  pub struct ServeArgs {
  363      /// Address for the server to listen on
  364      #[arg(long, default_value = "127.0.0.1:2419")]
  365      pub bind: SocketAddr,
  366      /// Secret token for client authentication (auto-generated if not provided)
  367      #[arg(long, env = "GROK_AGENT_SECRET")]
  368      pub secret: Option<String>,
  369      /// Remote agent URL for proxy mode
  370      #[arg(long)]
  371      pub remote: Option<String>,
  372      /// Authentication and WebSocket URL overrides
  373      #[command(flatten)]
  374      pub headless: HeadlessArgs,
  375  }
  376  impl ServeArgs {
  377      /// Get the secret, generating a random one if not provided.
  378      pub fn get_secret(&self) -> String {
  379          self.secret
  380              .clone()
  381              .unwrap_or_else(|| generate_random_key(12))
  382      }
  383  }
  384  /// Generate a random alphanumeric key of the given length.
  385  fn generate_random_key(len: usize) -> String {
  386      let raw = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string().replace('-', "");
  387      raw.chars().cycle().take(len).collect()
  388  }
  389  /// Arguments for the `agent leader` subcommand.
  390  #[derive(Debug, clap::Args, Clone)]
  391  pub struct LeaderArgs {
  392      /// Keep the leader running after the last client disconnects.
  393      #[arg(long)]

  395      /// Defer the grok.com relay WebSocket until the first headless IPC client

  399      /// from interactive clients (TUI/IDE), which only need the relay if a
  400      /// headless client appears.
  401      #[arg(long)]
  402      pub relay_on_demand: bool,

//! ACP-advertised commands appear in the dropdown but pass through to the

/acp→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
    1  //! Wrapper that turns an ACP `AvailableCommand` into a `SlashCommand`.
    2  //!
    3  //! ACP-advertised commands appear in the dropdown but pass through to the
    4  //! shell for execution. The wrapper stores `String` fields -- consistent
    5  //! with the `&str` trait design.
    6  //!
    7  //! Skills (`SkillMeta::Skill`) are also passed through as `/name args` for
    8  //! the shell to expand, but marked `InjectSkill` for rendering.
    9
   10  use agent_client_protocol as acp;
   11  use xai_grok_tools::implementations::skills::types::SkillScope;
   12
   13  use super::command::{CommandExecCtx, CommandProvenance, CommandResult, SlashCommand};
   14
   15  /// Identity of a skill as advertised in ACP `_meta`.
   16  #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
   17  pub struct SkillIdentity {
   18      pub path: String,
   19      pub scope: SkillScope,
   20      /// Plugin install name when present (`acme`).
   21      pub plugin_name: Option<String>,
   22  }
   23
   24  impl SkillIdentity {
   25      /// Plugin install name, else the scope (plugin skills can carry any scope).
   26      pub fn source(&self) -> &str {
   27          self.plugin_name.as_deref().unwrap_or(self.scope.as_ref())
   28      }
   29  }
   30
   31  /// Parsed ACP `_meta` skill fields.
   32  #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
   33  pub enum SkillMeta {
   34      /// No skill keys.
   35      Absent,
   36      Skill(SkillIdentity),
   37      /// Unknown `scope` string (e.g. `"workflow"`). Pass through, don't error.
   38      Foreign,
   39      /// Skill-like keys present but invalid. Invocation errors rather than
   40      /// silently degrading.
   41      Malformed,
   42  }
   43
   44  impl SkillMeta {
   45      pub fn parse(meta: Option<&serde_json::Map<String, serde_json::Value>>) -> Self {
   46          let Some(m) = meta else {
   47              return SkillMeta::Absent;
   48          };
   49          let path_val = m.get("path");
   50          let scope_val = m.get("scope");
   51          if path_val.is_none() && scope_val.is_none() {
   52              return SkillMeta::Absent;
   53          }
   54          let path = path_val.and_then(|v| v.as_str());
   55          let scope: Option<SkillScope> =
   56              scope_val.and_then(|v| serde_json::from_value(v.clone()).ok());
   57          match (path, scope) {
   58              (Some(path), Some(scope)) => SkillMeta::Skill(SkillIdentity {
   59                  path: path.to_string(),
   60                  scope,
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

  692      #[test]
  693      #[should_panic(expected = "alias")]
  694      fn registry_panics_on_builtin_alias_collision() {
  695          let cmd_a: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  696              name: "alpha",
  697              aliases: &["dup"],
  698          });
  699          let cmd_b: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  700              name: "beta",
  701              aliases: &["dup"],
  702          });
  703          let _ = CommandRegistry::new(vec![cmd_a, cmd_b]);
  704      }
  705
  706      #[test]
  707      fn trigger_count_includes_aliases() {
  708          let cmd: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  709              name: "exit",
  710              aliases: &["quit", "q"],
  711          });
  712          let registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![cmd]);
  713          // 1 canonical + 2 aliases = 3 triggers.
  714          assert_eq!(registry.triggers().len(), 3);
  715          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 1);
  716      }
  717
  718      #[test]
  719      fn set_acp_commands_replaces_only_acp() {
  720          let builtin: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  721              name: "exit",
  722              aliases: &["quit"],
  723          });
  724          let mut registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![builtin]);
  725          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 1);
  726
  727          // Add ACP commands.
  728          let acp_cmds = vec![agent_client_protocol::AvailableCommand::new(
  729              "flush".to_string(),
  730              "Flush memory".to_string(),
  731          )];
  732          registry.set_acp_commands(&acp_cmds);
  733          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 2);
  734          assert!(registry.get("flush").is_some());
  735
  736          // Replace ACP commands -- flush should be gone, builtin stays.
  737          registry.set_acp_commands(&[]);
  738          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 1);
  739          assert!(registry.get("exit").is_some());
  740          assert!(registry.get("flush").is_none());
  741      }

 1204          reg.set_auto_mode_available(true);
 1205
 1206          reg.set_menu_hidden_for_test("always-approve", true);
 1207          assert!(
 1208              reg.get("always-approve").is_none(),
 1209              "menu lookup hides the command"
 1210          );

 1212              !reg.triggers()

 1215              "no completion trigger while menu-hidden"
 1216          );

 1218              reg.get_for_dispatch("always-approve").is_some(),
 1219              "typed invocation must still resolve for dispatch"
 1220          );
 1221          assert!(
 1222              reg.get_for_dispatch("yolo").is_some(),
 1223              "aliases of a menu-hidden command must still resolve for dispatch"
 1224          );
 1225          // Bystanders unaffected.
 1226          assert!(reg.get("exit").is_some());
 1227          assert!(reg.get("auto").is_some());
 1228

 1230          assert!(reg.get("always-approve").is_some());

 1232              reg.triggers()
 1233                  .iter()
 1234                  .any(|t| t.canonical == "always-approve")
 1235          );

    1  //! `/announcements` -- show or hide the announcement banner.
    2
    3  use crate::app::actions::Action;
    4  use crate::slash::command::{AppCtx, ArgItem, CommandExecCtx, CommandResult, SlashCommand};
    5
    6  const USAGE: &str = "Usage: /announcements hide | show";
    7
    8  /// Control the announcement banner (hide/show).
    9  pub struct AnnouncementsCommand;
   10
   11  impl SlashCommand for AnnouncementsCommand {
   12      fn name(&self) -> &str {
   13          "announcements"
   14      }
   15
   16      fn description(&self) -> &str {
   17          "Show or hide announcements"
   18      }
   19
   20      fn usage(&self) -> &str {
   21          "/announcements hide | show"
   22      }
   23
   24      fn takes_args(&self) -> bool {
   25          true
   26      }
   27
   28      fn args_required(&self) -> bool {
   29          true
   30      }
   31
   32      fn arg_placeholder(&self) -> Option<&str> {
   33          Some("hide|show")
   34      }
   35
   36      fn suggest_args(&self, _ctx: &AppCtx, _args_query: &str) -> Option<Vec<ArgItem>> {
   37          Some(vec![
   38              ArgItem {
   39                  display: "hide".to_string(),
   40                  match_text: "hide".to_string(),
   41                  insert_text: "hide".to_string(),
   42                  description: "Hide the announcement banner".to_string(),
   43              },
   44              ArgItem {
   45                  display: "show".to_string(),
   46                  match_text: "show".to_string(),
   47                  insert_text: "show".to_string(),
   48                  description: "Show the announcement banner".to_string(),
   49              },
   50          ])
   51      }
   52
   53      fn visible(&self, ctx: &AppCtx) -> bool {
   54          ctx.has_session_announcements
   55      }
   56
   57      fn run(&self, _ctx: &mut CommandExecCtx, args: &str) -> CommandResult {
   58          match args.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("") {
   59              "hide" => CommandResult::Action(Action::AnnouncementsHide),
   60              "show" => CommandResult::Action(Action::AnnouncementsShow),
   61              _ => CommandResult::Error(USAGE.into()),
   62          }
   63      }

  723          });

  727          // Add ACP commands.

  729              "flush".to_string(),
  730              "Flush memory".to_string(),
  731          )];
  732          registry.set_acp_commands(&acp_cmds);
  733          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 2);
  734          assert!(registry.get("flush").is_some());
  735
  736          // Replace ACP commands -- flush should be gone, builtin stays.
  737          registry.set_acp_commands(&[]);
  738          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 1);
  739          assert!(registry.get("exit").is_some());
  740          assert!(registry.get("flush").is_none());

/compact→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
    1  //! `/compact` -- compact conversation history.
    2  //!
    3  //! Takes an optional context argument. Stays on the existing queue pipeline:
    4  //! returns `CommandResult::QueueCommand` so the dispatch layer enqueues it
    5  //! as `QueueEntryKind::Command`.
    6
    7  use crate::slash::command::{CommandExecCtx, CommandResult, SlashCommand};
    8
    9  /// Compact the conversation history, optionally with a focus context.
   10  pub struct CompactCommand;
   11
   12  impl SlashCommand for CompactCommand {
   13      fn name(&self) -> &str {
   14          "compact"
   15      }
   16
   17      fn description(&self) -> &str {
   18          "Compact conversation history"
   19      }
   20
   21      fn session_scoped(&self) -> bool {
   22          true
   23      }
   24
   25      fn usage(&self) -> &str {
   26          "/compact compaction instructions"
   27      }
   28
   29      fn takes_args(&self) -> bool {
   30          true
   31      }
   32
   33      /// Args are optional -- `/compact` with no args is valid.
   34      fn args_required(&self) -> bool {
   35          false
   36      }
   37
   38      fn arg_placeholder(&self) -> Option<&str> {
   39          Some("compaction instructions")
   40      }
   41
   42      fn run(&self, _ctx: &mut CommandExecCtx, args: &str) -> CommandResult {
   43          // Re-emit as queue command, preserving the full text.
   44          let text = if args.trim().is_empty() {
   45              "/compact".to_string()
   46          } else {
   47              format!("/compact {}", args)
   48          };
   49          CommandResult::QueueCommand(text)
   50      }
   51  }
   52

! - `/copy 2 out.txt` — 2nd-latest → file

/copy→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
    7  //! - `/copy 2 out.txt` — 2nd-latest → file
    8
    9  use std::path::PathBuf;
   10
   11  use crate::app::actions::Action;
   12  use crate::slash::command::{CommandExecCtx, CommandResult, SlashCommand};
   13
   14  /// Copy an assistant message to the clipboard (or an optional file).
   15  pub struct CopyCommand;
   16
   17  impl SlashCommand for CopyCommand {
   18      fn name(&self) -> &str {
   19          "copy"
   20      }
   21
   22      fn description(&self) -> &str {
   23          "Copy last response to clipboard or file (/copy [N] [file])"
   24      }
   25
   26      fn session_scoped(&self) -> bool {
   27          true
   28      }
   29
   30      fn usage(&self) -> &str {
   31          "/copy [N] [file]"
   32      }
   33
   34      fn takes_args(&self) -> bool {
   35          true
   36      }
   37
   38      fn arg_placeholder(&self) -> Option<&str> {
   39          Some("[N] [file]")
   40      }
   41
   42      fn run(&self, _ctx: &mut CommandExecCtx, args: &str) -> CommandResult {
   43          match parse_copy_args(args) {
   44              Ok((n, file_path)) => {
   45                  CommandResult::Action(Action::CopyAssistantMessage { n, file_path })
   46              }
   47              Err(msg) => CommandResult::Error(msg),
   48          }
   49      }
   50  }
   51
   52  /// Parse `/copy` args into `(n, optional_file_path)`.
   53  ///
   54  /// - empty → `(1, None)`
   55  /// - `2` → `(2, None)`
   56  /// - `out.txt` → `(1, Some(out.txt))`
   57  /// - `2 out.txt` → `(2, Some(out.txt))` (rest of line is the path, spaces ok)
   58  fn parse_copy_args(args: &str) -> Result<(usize, Option<PathBuf>), String> {
   59      let trimmed = args.trim();
   60      if trimmed.is_empty() {
   61          return Ok((1, None));
   62      }
   63
   64      let mut parts = trimmed.splitn(2, char::is_whitespace);
   65      let first = parts.next().unwrap_or("");
   66      let rest = parts.next().map(str::trim).filter(|s| !s.is_empty());
   67
   68      match first.parse::<usize>() {
   69          Ok(0) => Err("Usage: /copy [N] [file] where N is 1 (latest), 2, 3, ...".to_string()),
   70          Ok(n) => Ok((n, rest.map(PathBuf::from))),
   71          Err(_) => {
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

  828              name: "usage",
  829              aliases: &["cost"],
  830          });

  832          assert!(!registry.is_restricted("usage"), "empty deny list");
  833
  834          registry.set_restricted_commands(&["usage".to_string()]);
  835          assert!(registry.get("usage").is_none(), "hidden from get()");
  836          assert!(registry.is_restricted("usage"));
  837          assert!(registry.is_restricted("cost"), "alias resolves");
  838          assert!(registry.is_restricted("/Usage"), "normalized lookup");
  839          assert!(!registry.is_restricted("frobnicate"), "unknown name");
  840      }

  844          let cmd: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  845              name: "exit",
  846              aliases: &["quit"],
  847          });
  848          let mut registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![cmd]);
  849
  850          // Denying an alias hides the command entirely (canonical too).
  851          registry.set_restricted_commands(&["quit".to_string()]);
  852          assert!(registry.get("exit").is_none());
  853          assert!(registry.get("quit").is_none());
  854
  855          // Denying the canonical name also hides alias lookups.
  856          registry.set_restricted_commands(&["exit".to_string()]);
  857          assert!(registry.get("exit").is_none());
  858          assert!(registry.get("quit").is_none());
  859      }

  866          });

  868
  869          registry.set_restricted_commands(&["share".to_string()]);
  870          // A later `set_share_visible(true)` must NOT resurrect a
  871          // restricted command — deny wins over every visibility gate.
  872          registry.set_share_visible(true);

  901      fn dashboard_command_hidden_by_default_and_toggleable() {
  902          let dashboard: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  903              name: "dashboard",
  904              aliases: &[],
  905          });
  906          let other: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  907              name: "exit",
  908              aliases: &[],
  909          });
  910          let mut registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![dashboard, other]);
  911
  912          // Fail-closed: hidden by default (until the feature flag reveals it).
  913          assert!(registry.get("dashboard").is_none());
  914          assert!(
  915              !registry
  916                  .triggers()
  917                  .iter()
  918                  .any(|t| t.canonical == "dashboard")
  919          );
  920          // Unrelated commands are unaffected.
  921          assert!(registry.get("exit").is_some());
  922
  923          // Enabling the feature reveals it.
  924          registry.set_dashboard_visible(true);
  925          assert!(registry.get("dashboard").is_some());
  926          assert!(
  927              registry
  928                  .triggers()
  929                  .iter()
  930                  .any(|t| t.canonical == "dashboard")
  931          );
  932
  933          // Hiding again removes it.
  934          registry.set_dashboard_visible(false);
  935          assert!(registry.get("dashboard").is_none());
  936      }

    3  //! Runs the shared TUI probe and diagnostics path, including live runtime
    4  //! evidence that the standalone command cannot observe.

    7      AppCtx, ArgItem, CommandExecCtx, CommandResult, DoctorRequest, SlashCommand,

   19          runtime: crate::diagnostics::TuiRuntimeRequest<'_>,
   20      ) -> crate::diagnostics::DiagnosticReport {
   21          let query = crate::diagnostics::probes::LiveTmuxProbe;
   22          let snapshot = crate::diagnostics::probes::collect_doctor_tui(

   35              runtime.notification_condition,
   36              runtime.workspace,
   37          );
   38          let mut report = crate::diagnostics::view(snapshot.into());
   39          crate::diagnostics::merge_tui_runtime_findings(&mut report, runtime_findings);
   40          report
   41      }
   42  }

   49          if items.is_empty() { None } else { Some(items) }
   50      }
   51
   52      fn run(&self, ctx: &mut CommandExecCtx, args: &str) -> CommandResult {

   59              None
   60          } else {
   61              Some(PathBuf::from(trimmed))
   62          };

   65      }

/fork

交互 slash 指令 · command module boundary · crates/codegen/xai-grok-pager/src/slash/commands/fork.rs:14
源码 ↗

! not in this command itself.

/fork→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
    9  //! The actual fork dispatch happens in `dispatch_fork_resolved` (after
   10  //! the worktree question is resolved and the placeholder spawn succeeds),
   11  //! not in this command itself.
   12
   13  use crate::app::actions::Action;
   14  use crate::slash::command::{CommandExecCtx, CommandResult, SlashCommand};
   15
   16  /// Parsed arguments for the `/fork` slash command.
   17  ///
   18  /// Returned by [`parse_fork_args`] and carried in
   19  /// [`Action::Fork`](crate::app::actions::Action::Fork) for the
   20  /// dispatcher to consume.
   21  #[derive(Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
   22  pub struct ForkArgs {
   23      /// `None`        -> open the worktree question modal (the user is
   24      ///                  asked every time; the choice is never persisted).
   25      /// `Some(true)`  -> force worktree, skipping the modal.
   26      /// `Some(false)` -> force no-worktree, skipping the modal.
   27      pub worktree_override: Option<bool>,
   28      /// Optional first prompt for the new session. Whitespace-trimmed.
   29      /// `None` when the user invoked `/fork` (with or without flags) but
   30      /// no directive text. The new agent simply opens with no first prompt.
   31      pub directive: Option<String>,
   32  }
   33
   34  /// Parse the raw argument string after `/fork`.
   35  ///
   36  /// Recognised flags appear at the start; everything after the last flag
   37  /// is the directive. Unknown flags are deliberately treated as the
   38  /// start of the directive (so `/fork --foo bar` becomes a directive
   39  /// `--foo bar`) -- the parser is conservative because the args are
   40  /// user-typed text and we do not want to reject directives that happen
   41  /// to begin with `--`.
   42  ///
   43  /// Errors:
   44  /// - `--worktree` and `--no-worktree` cannot both appear.
   45  /// - `--at <turn>` returns a friendly "not supported in this version"
   46  ///   message: the shell already supports the underlying parameter (see
   47  ///   `xai_grok_shell::session::fork::ForkSessionRequest::target_prompt_index`)
   48  ///   and a turn-picker UI is planned; this version deliberately rejects
   49  ///   the flag so users discover the deferral cleanly.
   50  pub fn parse_fork_args(args: &str) -> Result<ForkArgs, String> {
   51      let mut worktree_override: Option<bool> = None;
   52      let mut rest = args.trim_start();
   53
   54      while !rest.is_empty() {
   55          let (flag, after) = match rest.split_once(char::is_whitespace) {
   56              Some(parts) => parts,
   57              None => (rest, ""),
   58          };
   59          match flag {
   60              "--worktree" => {
   61                  if worktree_override == Some(false) {
   62                      return Err("--worktree and --no-worktree are mutually exclusive".into());
   63                  }
   64                  if worktree_override == Some(true) {
   65                      return Err("--worktree specified twice".into());
   66                  }
   67                  worktree_override = Some(true);
   68                  rest = after.trim_start();
   69              }
   70              "--no-worktree" => {
   71                  if worktree_override == Some(true) {
   72                      return Err("--worktree and --no-worktree are mutually exclusive".into());
   73                  }
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

    1  //! `/gboom` -- hidden easter egg. Launches a tiny raycaster shooter
    2  //! rendered through the kitty graphics protocol.
    3  //!
    4  //! Never listed in the slash dropdown (`visible()` is false) but executes
    5  //! when typed exactly as `/gboom`; with any argument it passes through to
    6  //! the shell like an unknown command, so only the bare invocation triggers.
    7
    8  use crate::app::actions::Action;
    9  use crate::slash::command::{AppCtx, CommandExecCtx, CommandResult, SlashCommand};
   10
   11  /// Hidden GBOOM easter egg.

   15      fn name(&self) -> &str {
   16          "gboom"
   17      }
   18
   19      fn description(&self) -> &str {
   20          // Never shown: the command is hidden from the dropdown.
   21          "Hidden easter egg"
   22      }
   23
   24      fn usage(&self) -> &str {
   25          "/gboom"
   26      }
   27
   28      /// Easter egg: typeable, never listed.
   29      fn visible(&self, _ctx: &AppCtx) -> bool {
   30          false
   31      }
   32
   33      /// Needs an agent view to render in.
   34      fn session_scoped(&self) -> bool {
   35          true
   36      }
   37
   38      fn run(&self, _ctx: &mut CommandExecCtx, args: &str) -> CommandResult {
   39          if !args.trim().is_empty() {
   40              // With arguments, behave as if the command didn't exist:
   41              // forward the text to the shell/model with the args untouched.
   42              return CommandResult::PassThrough(format!("/gboom {args}"));
   43          }
   44          CommandResult::Action(Action::OpenGboom)
   45      }
   46  }

  940      fn login_builtin() -> Arc<dyn SlashCommand> {
  941          Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  942              name: "login",

  944          })
  945      }
  946
  947      fn acp_skill(name: &str, meta: serde_json::Value) -> agent_client_protocol::AvailableCommand {
  948          agent_client_protocol::AvailableCommand::new(name.to_string(), format!("{name} skill"))
  949              .meta(meta.as_object().cloned().unwrap())
  950      }

  952      #[test]
  953      fn advertised_qualified_skill_sits_beside_builtin() {
  954          let mut registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![login_builtin()]);
  955          registry.set_acp_commands(&[acp_skill(
  956              "acme:login",
  957              serde_json::json!({
  958                  "scope": "plugin",
  959                  "path": "/x/SKILL.md",
  960                  "pluginName": "acme",
  961              }),
  962          )]);
  963
  964          assert!(registry.is_builtin("login"));
  965          assert_eq!(
  966              registry.get("login").unwrap().provenance(),
  967              CommandProvenance::Builtin
  968          );
  969          let skill = registry.get("acme:login").expect("qualified skill");
  970          assert!(!registry.is_builtin("acme:login"));
  971          assert_eq!(
  972              skill.provenance(),
  973              CommandProvenance::Skill {
  974                  source: "acme".to_string()
  975              }
  976          );
  977          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 2);
  978
  979          let skill_match_texts: HashSet<&str> = registry
  980              .triggers()
  981              .iter()
  982              .filter(|t| t.canonical == "acme:login")
  983              .inspect(|t| assert_eq!(t.display, "/acme:login"))
  984              .map(|t| t.match_text.as_str())
  985              .collect();
  986          assert_eq!(skill_match_texts, HashSet::from(["login", "acme:login"]));
  987      }

/// module-level constant so the trait method can return a `'static`

/loop-cmd→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执

    1  use agent_client_protocol as acp;

    6  use crate::slash::command::{CommandExecCtx, CommandResult, ScheduledTaskPreview, SlashCommand};
    7
    8  /// Pre-built slice for `LoopCommand::required_tools()`. Lifted to a
    9  /// module-level constant so the trait method can return a `'static`
   10  /// slice; the constant pulls the canonical name from `xai-grok-tools`
   11  /// so a tool rename surfaces here at compile time.
   12  const LOOP_REQUIRED_TOOLS: &[&str] = &[SCHEDULER_CREATE_TOOL_NAME];
   13
   14  pub struct LoopCommand;
   15
   16  /// Split `/loop` args into an optional leading compact interval token (only for
   17  /// seeding the provisional preview) and the prompt. Returns `Some(token)` only
   18  /// for a `^\d+[smhd]$` first token followed by prompt text; otherwise `None`,
   19  /// leaving the model to derive the real interval. There is no host-side default.
   20  fn parse_loop_args(args: &str) -> (Option<&str>, &str) {
   21      let trimmed = args.trim();
   22      if let Some(space) = trimmed.find(char::is_whitespace) {
   23          let first = &trimmed[..space];
   24          let rest = trimmed[space..].trim_start();
   25          if is_interval_token(first) && !rest.is_empty() {
   26              return (Some(first), rest);
   27          }
   28      }
   29      (None, trimmed)
   30  }
   31
   32  /// Whether a token is a schedulable interval: non-zero digits followed by one
   33  /// of s/m/h/d. Zero is rejected so the preview never shows a cadence the tool
   34  /// would reject (`parse_interval` errors on zero).
   35  fn is_interval_token(s: &str) -> bool {
   36      if s.len() < 2 {
   37          return false;
   38      }
   39      let (digits, suffix) = s.split_at(s.len() - 1);
   40      matches!(suffix, "s" | "m" | "h" | "d")
   41          && digits.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit())
   42          && digits.parse::<u64>().is_ok_and(|n| n > 0)
   43  }
   44
   45  /// Convert an interval token like "5m" to a human string like "every 5 minutes".
   46  fn interval_to_human(token: &str) -> String {
   47      let (digits, suffix) = token.split_at(token.len() - 1);
   48      let n: u64 = digits.parse().unwrap_or(0);
   49      match suffix {
   50          "s" => {
   51              if n <= 1 {
   52                  "every 1 second".into()
   53              } else {
   54                  format!("every {n} seconds")
   55              }
   56          }
   57          "m" => {
   58              if n == 1 {
   59                  "every 1 minute".into()
   60              } else {
   61                  format!("every {n} minutes")
   62              }
   63          }
   64          "h" => {
   65              if n == 1 {
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

//! wrapper around nucleo's `MultiPattern` that provides ranked results

/matcher→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
    1  //! Nucleo-based fuzzy matcher for slash command and argument suggestions.
    2  //!
    3  //! Thin
    4  //! wrapper around nucleo's `MultiPattern` that provides ranked results
    5  //! and highlight index extraction.
    6
    7  use nucleo::{
    8      Config, Matcher, Utf32String,
    9      pattern::{CaseMatching, MultiPattern, Normalization},
   10  };
   11
   12  /// Fuzzy matcher backed by nucleo.
   13  ///
   14  /// Maintains internal state (pattern + matcher) between calls for efficiency.
   15  /// Not thread-safe -- intended for single-threaded use within `SlashController`.
   16  #[derive(Debug)]
   17  pub struct FuzzyMatcher {
   18      pattern: MultiPattern,
   19      matcher: Matcher,
   20  }
   21
   22  impl Default for FuzzyMatcher {
   23      fn default() -> Self {
   24          Self::new()
   25      }
   26  }
   27
   28  impl FuzzyMatcher {
   29      pub fn new() -> Self {
   30          Self {
   31              pattern: MultiPattern::new(1),
   32              matcher: Matcher::new(Config::DEFAULT),
   33          }
   34      }
   35
   36      /// Rank items by fuzzy match score.
   37      ///
   38      /// Returns `(index, score)` pairs sorted by descending score, then
   39      /// ascending key text. At most `limit` results are returned.
   40      ///
   41      /// When `query` is empty, returns the first `limit` items with score 0
   42      /// (insertion order).
   43      pub fn rank<T, F>(
   44          &mut self,
   45          items: &[T],
   46          query: &str,
   47          limit: usize,
   48          mut key_fn: F,
   49      ) -> Vec<(usize, u32)>
   50      where
   51          F: FnMut(&T) -> &str,
   52      {
   53          if limit == 0 || items.is_empty() {
   54              return Vec::new();
   55          }
   56
   57          let trimmed = query.trim();
   58          if trimmed.is_empty() {
   59              let capped = items.len().min(limit);
   60              return (0..capped).map(|idx| (idx, 0)).collect();
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

 1160      fn multi_tool_command_requires_all_tools() {
 1161          let gated: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(ToolGatedCommand {
 1162              name: "multi",
 1163              required: &["a", "b"],
 1164          });

 1166

  684          assert!(registry.get("quit").is_some());
  685          // Both resolve to the same command.
  686          assert!(std::ptr::eq(
  687              registry.get("exit").unwrap().as_ref() as *const dyn SlashCommand,
  688              registry.get("quit").unwrap().as_ref() as *const dyn SlashCommand,
  689          ));
  690      }
  691
  692      #[test]
  693      #[should_panic(expected = "alias")]
  694      fn registry_panics_on_builtin_alias_collision() {
  695          let cmd_a: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  696              name: "alpha",
  697              aliases: &["dup"],
  698          });
  699          let cmd_b: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  700              name: "beta",
  701              aliases: &["dup"],
  702          });
  703          let _ = CommandRegistry::new(vec![cmd_a, cmd_b]);
  704      }
  705
  706      #[test]
  707      fn trigger_count_includes_aliases() {
  708          let cmd: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  709              name: "exit",
  710              aliases: &["quit", "q"],
  711          });
  712          let registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![cmd]);
  713          // 1 canonical + 2 aliases = 3 triggers.
  714          assert_eq!(registry.triggers().len(), 3);
  715          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 1);
  716      }
  717
  718      #[test]
  719      fn set_acp_commands_replaces_only_acp() {
  720          let builtin: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  721              name: "exit",
  722              aliases: &["quit"],
  723          });
  724          let mut registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![builtin]);
  725          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 1);
  726
  727          // Add ACP commands.
  728          let acp_cmds = vec![agent_client_protocol::AvailableCommand::new(
  729              "flush".to_string(),
  730              "Flush memory".to_string(),
  731          )];
  732          registry.set_acp_commands(&acp_cmds);
  733          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 2);
  734          assert!(registry.get("flush").is_some());
  735
  736          // Replace ACP commands -- flush should be gone, builtin stays.
  737          registry.set_acp_commands(&[]);
  738          assert_eq!(registry.command_count(), 1);
  739          assert!(registry.get("exit").is_some());

! `/rename` (alias `/title`) -- rename the current session.

/rename→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
    1  //! `/rename` (alias `/title`) -- rename the current session.
    2
    3  use crate::app::actions::Action;
    4  use crate::slash::command::{AppCtx, ArgItem, CommandExecCtx, CommandResult, SlashCommand};
    5  use xai_grok_shell::session::persistence::{MAX_TITLE_SCALARS, sanitize_rename_title};
    6
    7  /// Rename the current session's title/summary.
    8  pub struct RenameCommand;
    9
   10  impl SlashCommand for RenameCommand {
   11      fn name(&self) -> &str {
   12          "rename"
   13      }
   14
   15      fn aliases(&self) -> &[&str] {
   16          &["title"]
   17      }
   18
   19      fn description(&self) -> &str {
   20          "Rename the current session"
   21      }
   22
   23      fn session_scoped(&self) -> bool {
   24          true
   25      }
   26
   27      fn usage(&self) -> &str {
   28          "/rename <title> | --auto"
   29      }
   30
   31      fn takes_args(&self) -> bool {
   32          true
   33      }
   34
   35      fn args_required(&self) -> bool {
   36          true
   37      }
   38
   39      fn arg_placeholder(&self) -> Option<&str> {
   40          Some("<title>")
   41      }
   42
   43      fn suggest_args(&self, ctx: &AppCtx, args_query: &str) -> Option<Vec<ArgItem>> {
   44          // Empty-args-only: a ghost row that survives typed input (including
   45          // `--auto`) steals Enter (`accept_slash_completion` replaces the
   46          // args range).
   47          if !args_query.trim().is_empty() {
   48              return None;
   49          }
   50          // Prefill is `rename_source_title`, not `entry_title` — see that
   51          // helper. `current_title` is already sanitized at sync time.
   52          let title = ctx.current_title?.trim();
   53          if title.is_empty() || title == "--auto" {
   54              return None;
   55          }
   56          Some(vec![ArgItem {
   57              display: title.to_owned(),
   58              match_text: title.to_owned(),
   59              insert_text: title.to_owned(),
   60              description: "current title".to_string(),
   61          }])
   62      }
   63

    8  pub struct ScreenModeSwitchCommand {

   12  }
   13
   14  impl ScreenModeSwitchCommand {
   15      /// `/minimal`: offered in the full TUI (alt-screen or `--no-alt-screen`
   16      /// inline), relaunches with `--minimal`.
   17      pub const fn minimal() -> Self {
   18          Self { to_minimal: true }
   19      }
   20
   21      /// `/fullscreen` (alias `/full`): offered in minimal, relaunches without
   22      /// `--minimal`.
   23      pub const fn fullscreen() -> Self {
   24          Self { to_minimal: false }
   25      }
   26
   27      fn target_label(&self) -> &'static str {
   28          if self.to_minimal {
   29              "minimal"
   30          } else {
   31              "fullscreen"
   32          }
   33      }
   34  }
   35

   37      fn name(&self) -> &str {
   38          self.target_label()
   39      }
   40
   41      fn aliases(&self) -> &[&str] {
   42          if self.to_minimal { &[] } else { &["full"] }
   43      }
   44
   45      fn description(&self) -> &str {
   46          if self.to_minimal {
   47              "Reopen this session in minimal (scrollback-native) mode — switch back with /fullscreen"
   48          } else {
   49              "Reopen this session in fullscreen mode — switch back with /minimal"
   50          }
   51      }
   52
   53      fn usage(&self) -> &str {
   54          if self.to_minimal {
   55              "/minimal"
   56          } else {
   57              "/fullscreen"
   58          }
   59      }
   60
   61      fn session_scoped(&self) -> bool {
   62          true
   63      }

/share

交互 slash 指令 · named command catalog · crates/codegen/xai-grok-pager/src/slash/registry.rs:746
源码 ↗

aliases: &[],

/share→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  741      }
  742
  743      #[test]
  744      fn set_share_visible_hides_and_restores_share_command() {
  745          let share: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  746              name: "share",
  747              aliases: &[],
  748          });
  749          let other: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  750              name: "exit",
  751              aliases: &[],
  752          });
  753          let mut registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![share, other]);
  754
  755          // Default: /share is menu-hidden (offered nowhere) but still dispatchable.
  756          assert!(registry.get("share").is_none());
  757          assert!(
  758              registry.get_for_dispatch("share").is_some(),
  759              "typed /share must still resolve while menu-hidden"
  760          );
  761          assert!(!registry.triggers().iter().any(|t| t.canonical == "share"));
  762
  763          // Revealing /share restores menu lookup and triggers.
  764          registry.set_share_visible(true);
  765          assert!(registry.get("share").is_some());
  766          assert!(registry.get_for_dispatch("share").is_some());
  767          assert!(registry.triggers().iter().any(|t| t.canonical == "share"));
  768          // Other commands are unaffected.
  769          assert!(registry.get("exit").is_some());
  770
  771          // Hiding again is menu-only: no offer, typed path still works.
  772          registry.set_share_visible(false);
  773          assert!(registry.get("share").is_none());
  774          assert!(registry.get_for_dispatch("share").is_some());
  775          assert!(!registry.triggers().iter().any(|t| t.canonical == "share"));
  776      }
  777
  778      #[test]
  779      fn restricted_commands_hide_and_restore() {
  780          let usage: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  781              name: "usage",
  782              aliases: &[],
  783          });
  784          let other: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  785              name: "exit",
  786              aliases: &[],
  787          });
  788          let mut registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![usage, other]);
  789          assert!(registry.get("usage").is_some());
  790
  791          registry.set_restricted_commands(&["usage".to_string()]);
  792          // Execution is blocked …
  793          assert!(registry.get("usage").is_none());
  794          // … but the command stays listed (dropdown/completion
  795          // discoverability — invoking shows the upsell instead).
  796          assert!(registry.triggers().iter().any(|t| t.canonical == "usage"));
  797          // Other commands unaffected.
  798          assert!(registry.get("exit").is_some());
  799          assert_eq!(registry.restricted_commands(), vec!["usage"]);
  800
  801          // Clearing the deny list restores execution.
  802          registry.set_restricted_commands(&[]);
  803          assert!(registry.get("usage").is_some());
  804          assert!(registry.restricted_commands().is_empty());
  805      }
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

   27      }

   31      }

   56          true
   57      }
   58

   60          Some(Theme::current_kind().display_name().to_string())
   61      }
   62
   63      fn preview_arg(&self, arg: &str) {
   64          if let Some(kind) = ThemeKind::from_name(arg) {
   65              if kind.is_auto() {
   66                  // Preview the theme that auto mode would resolve to.
   67                  let resolved = theme_cache::resolve_auto();
   68                  Theme::apply_kind(resolved);
   69              } else {
   70                  Theme::apply_kind(kind);
   71              }
   72          }

  778      #[test]
  779      fn restricted_commands_hide_and_restore() {
  780          let usage: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  781              name: "usage",
  782              aliases: &[],
  783          });
  784          let other: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  785              name: "exit",
  786              aliases: &[],
  787          });
  788          let mut registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![usage, other]);
  789          assert!(registry.get("usage").is_some());
  790
  791          registry.set_restricted_commands(&["usage".to_string()]);
  792          // Execution is blocked …
  793          assert!(registry.get("usage").is_none());
  794          // … but the command stays listed (dropdown/completion
  795          // discoverability — invoking shows the upsell instead).
  796          assert!(registry.triggers().iter().any(|t| t.canonical == "usage"));
  797          // Other commands unaffected.
  798          assert!(registry.get("exit").is_some());
  799          assert_eq!(registry.restricted_commands(), vec!["usage"]);
  800
  801          // Clearing the deny list restores execution.
  802          registry.set_restricted_commands(&[]);
  803          assert!(registry.get("usage").is_some());
  804          assert!(registry.restricted_commands().is_empty());
  805      }
  806
  807      #[test]
  808      fn restricted_entries_are_normalized() {
  809          let usage: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  810              name: "usage",
  811              aliases: &[],
  812          });
  813          let mut registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![usage]);
  814
  815          // Leading slash, whitespace, and case are all tolerated; empty
  816          // entries are dropped rather than denying the "" name.
  817          registry.set_restricted_commands(&[" /Usage ".to_string(), String::new(), "/".to_string()]);
  818          assert!(registry.get("usage").is_none());
  819          assert_eq!(registry.restricted_commands(), vec!["usage"]);
  820      }
  821
  822      /// `is_restricted` scans the command list (not `key_to_index`, which
  823      /// can be missing tool-gated commands pre-handshake), resolves
  824      /// aliases, and never matches unknown names.
  825      #[test]
  826      fn is_restricted_resolves_names_and_aliases() {
  827          let usage: Arc<dyn SlashCommand> = Arc::new(DummyCommand {
  828              name: "usage",
  829              aliases: &["cost"],
  830          });
  831          let mut registry = CommandRegistry::new(vec![usage]);
  832          assert!(!registry.is_restricted("usage"), "empty deny list");
  833
  834          registry.set_restricted_commands(&["usage".to_string()]);
  835          assert!(registry.get("usage").is_none(), "hidden from get()");
  836          assert!(registry.is_restricted("usage"));
  837          assert!(registry.is_restricted("cost"), "alias resolves");
  838          assert!(registry.is_restricted("/Usage"), "normalized lookup");
  839          assert!(!registry.is_restricted("frobnicate"), "unknown name");
  840      }

   31          // configurable (toggle or hold via `voice_capture_mode`), so leave the
   32          // behavior unspecified.

   34              "Dictation (Ctrl+Space/F8; Esc/Enter to stop)"

   36              "Toggle dictation (Ctrl+Space/F8; Esc/Enter to stop)"

   46      /// the welcome screen) but still offered on the dashboard.
   47      fn session_scoped(&self) -> bool {
   48          true
   49      }
   50
   51      fn offered_when_session_less(&self) -> bool {
   52          true
   53      }
   54
   55      fn run(&self, _ctx: &mut CommandExecCtx, _args: &str) -> CommandResult {
   56          // Toggle, mirroring the voice key: starts dictation, or stops it
   57          // if already recording (Esc/Enter also stop).

   59      }

 1199      /// typed submission still resolves via `get_for_dispatch()` (including
 1200      /// aliases).
 1201      #[test]
 1202      fn menu_hidden_is_menu_only_and_still_dispatches() {
 1203          let mut reg = permission_mode_registry();
 1204          reg.set_auto_mode_available(true);
 1205
 1206          reg.set_menu_hidden_for_test("always-approve", true);
 1207          assert!(
 1208              reg.get("always-approve").is_none(),
 1209              "menu lookup hides the command"
 1210          );
 1211          assert!(
 1212              !reg.triggers()
 1213                  .iter()
 1214                  .any(|t| t.canonical == "always-approve"),
 1215              "no completion trigger while menu-hidden"
 1216          );
 1217          assert!(
 1218              reg.get_for_dispatch("always-approve").is_some(),
 1219              "typed invocation must still resolve for dispatch"
 1220          );
 1221          assert!(
 1222              reg.get_for_dispatch("yolo").is_some(),
 1223              "aliases of a menu-hidden command must still resolve for dispatch"
 1224          );
 1225          // Bystanders unaffected.
 1226          assert!(reg.get("exit").is_some());
 1227          assert!(reg.get("auto").is_some());
 1228
 1229          reg.set_menu_hidden_for_test("always-approve", false);
 1230          assert!(reg.get("always-approve").is_some());

 1232              reg.triggers()
 1233                  .iter()
 1234                  .any(|t| t.canonical == "always-approve")
 1235          );
 1236      }

02
DIMENSION · LOOP

控制循环

1 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factloop

01 · SessionActor 是事件驱动的长期存活 Actor

源码事实

run_session 同时监听命令、chat-state 事件、内部事件、模型切换、任务完成和多个定时器；启动时还建立文件监听、MCP liveness dispatcher 与 replay buffer。

白话解释

它不像一个简单 while 循环，更像一间控制室：用户输入、工具结果、文件变化、后台任务、模型切换都从不同通道进来，由同一个会话 Actor 排队处理。

对自研 Harness 的含义

这套 Harness 面向长会话、后台工作和 IDE/ACP 集成，控制面复杂度显著高于纯 CLI Agent。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 120–150
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 151–183
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 02d9359435d0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
crates/codegen/xai-grok-shell/src/session/acp_session_impl/run_loop.rs:120–183固定提交 ↗
  120  pub(super) async fn run_session(
  121      session: Arc<SessionActor>,
  122      mut cmd_rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<SessionCommand>,
  123      mut chat_state_event_rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<xai_chat_state::ChatStateEvent>,
  124      mut event_rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<SessionEvent>,
  125      fs_notify_config: Option<ClientFsConfig>,
  126      codebase_indexes: std::sync::Arc<parking_lot::Mutex<CodebaseIndexManager>>,
  127      index_root: std::path::PathBuf,
  128      fs_watch_caps: fs_watch::FsWatchCapabilities,
  129  ) {
  130      let (completion_tx, mut completion_rx) =
  131          mpsc::unbounded_channel::<(String, PromptTurnResult)>();
  132      tracing::debug!("fs_notify_config: {:?}", fs_notify_config);
  133      let mut replay_buffer = ReplayBuffer::new(session.buffering_settings.clone());
  134      let event_tx_for_flush_timer = session.event_tx.clone();
  135      let buffering_flush_interval = replay_buffer.max_wait_duration_ms();
  136      if let Some(buffering_flush_interval) = buffering_flush_interval {
  137          tokio::task::spawn_local(async move {
  138              let mut interval = tokio::time::interval(Duration::from_millis(std::cmp::max(
  139                  20,
  140                  buffering_flush_interval * 2,
  141              )));
  142              loop {
  143                  interval.tick().await;
  144                  let _ =
  145                      event_tx_for_flush_timer.send(SessionEvent::FlushReplay { respond_to: None });
  146              }
  147          });
  148      }
  149      let _workflow_watch = crate::config::watcher::ProjectDiscoveryWatcher::start(
  150          std::path::Path::new(session.session_info.cwd.as_str()),
  151      )
  152      .map(|(mut watcher, mut changes)| {
  153          let session = session.clone();
  154          tokio::task::spawn_local(async move {
  155              while let Some(change) = changes.recv().await {
  156                  watcher.refresh_new_dirs();
  157                  match change {
  158                      crate::config::watcher::DiscoveryChange::Skills => {
  159                          session.reload_skills_from_disk().await;
  160                      }
  161                      crate::config::watcher::DiscoveryChange::Workflows => {
  162                          session.send_available_commands_update().await;
  163                      }
  164                  }
  165              }
  166          })
  167      });
  168      let _fs_watch: Option<fs_watch::FsWatchHandle> = if fs_watch_caps.needs_watcher() {
  169          let deps = fs_watch::FsWatchDeps::from_session(
  170              &session,
  171              fs_notify_config.clone(),
  172              codebase_indexes.clone(),
  173              index_root.clone(),
  174          );
  175          tracing::debug!(?fs_watch_caps, "fs-notify: spawning");
  176          Some(fs_watch::spawn(fs_watch::FsWatchPlan::build(
  177              fs_watch_caps,
  178              deps,
  179          )))
  180      } else {
  181          tracing::debug!("fs-notify: skipped (no consumers)");
  182          None
  183      };

  135  async fn shutdown_workflows(session: &SessionActor) {
  136      if let Err(run_ids) = session
  137          .workflow_manager
  138          .lock()
  139          .await
  140          .cancel_all_and_drain(std::time::Duration::from_secs(7))
  141          .await
  142      {
  143          tracing::warn!(
  144              ?run_ids,
  145              "workflow shutdown completed with interrupted runs"
  146          );
  147      }
  148      let (respond_to, ack) = tokio::sync::oneshot::channel();
  149      if session
  150          .notifications
  151          .persistence_tx
  152          .send(PersistenceMsg::FlushAndAck { respond_to })
  153          .is_err()
  154      {
  155          tracing::warn!("workflow shutdown persistence channel closed before flush");
  156          return;
  157      }
  158      match tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(2), ack).await {
  159          Ok(Ok(Ok(()))) => {}
  160          Ok(Ok(Err(error))) => {
  161              tracing::warn!(%error, "workflow shutdown persistence flush failed")
  162          }
  163          Ok(Err(_)) => {
  164              tracing::warn!("workflow shutdown persistence actor dropped flush ack")
  165          }
  166          Err(_) => tracing::warn!("workflow shutdown persistence flush timed out"),
  167      }
  168  }
  169  pub(super) async fn run_session(
  170      session: Arc<SessionActor>,
  171      mut cmd_rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<SessionCommand>,
  172      mut chat_state_event_rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<xai_chat_state::ChatStateEvent>,
  173      mut event_rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<SessionEvent>,
  174      fs_notify_config: Option<ClientFsConfig>,
  175      codebase_indexes: std::sync::Arc<parking_lot::Mutex<CodebaseIndexManager>>,
  176      index_root: std::path::PathBuf,
  177      fs_watch_caps: fs_watch::FsWatchCapabilities,
  178  ) {
  179      let (completion_tx, mut completion_rx) =
  180          mpsc::unbounded_channel::<(String, PromptTurnResult)>();
  181      tracing::debug!("fs_notify_config: {:?}", fs_notify_config);
  182      let mut replay_buffer = ReplayBuffer::new(session.buffering_settings.clone());
  183      let event_tx_for_flush_timer = session.event_tx.clone();
  184      let buffering_flush_interval = replay_buffer.max_wait_duration_ms();
  185      if let Some(buffering_flush_interval) = buffering_flush_interval {
  186          tokio::task::spawn_local(async move {
  187              let mut interval = tokio::time::interval(Duration::from_millis(std::cmp::max(
  188                  20,
  189                  buffering_flush_interval * 2,
  190              )));
  191              loop {
  192                  interval.tick().await;
  193                  let _ =
  194                      event_tx_for_flush_timer.send(SessionEvent::FlushReplay { respond_to: None });
  195              }
  196          });
  197      }
  198      let _workflow_watch = crate::config::watcher::ProjectDiscoveryWatcher::start(
  199          std::path::Path::new(session.session_info.cwd.as_str()),
  200      )
  201      .map(|(mut watcher, mut changes)| {
  202          let session = session.clone();
  203          tokio::task::spawn_local(async move {
  204              while let Some(change) = changes.recv().await {
  205                  watcher.refresh_new_dirs();
  206                  match change {
  207                      crate::config::watcher::DiscoveryChange::Skills => {
  208                          session.reload_skills_from_disk().await;
  209                      }
  210                      crate::config::watcher::DiscoveryChange::Workflows => {
  211                          session.send_available_commands_update().await;
  212                      }
  213                  }
  214              }
  215          })
  216      });
  217      let _fs_watch: Option<fs_watch::FsWatchHandle> = if fs_watch_caps.needs_watcher() {
  218          let deps = fs_watch::FsWatchDeps::from_session(
  219              &session,
  220              fs_notify_config.clone(),
  221              codebase_indexes.clone(),
  222              index_root.clone(),
  223          );
  224          tracing::debug!(?fs_watch_caps, "fs-notify: spawning");
  225          Some(fs_watch::spawn(fs_watch::FsWatchPlan::build(
  226              fs_watch_caps,
  227              deps,
  228          )))
  229      } else {
  230          tracing::debug!("fs-notify: skipped (no consumers)");
  231          None
  232      };
  233      {
  234          let s = session.clone();
  235          tokio::task::spawn_local(async move { s.maybe_notify_git_branch().await });
  236      }
  237      let liveness_watchers_enabled = {
  238          let user_cfg = crate::config::load_effective_config().ok();
  239          let requirements = crate::agent::config::read_requirements_toml();
  240          crate::util::config::resolve_mcp_liveness_watchers(
  241              requirements.as_ref(),
  242              user_cfg.as_ref(),
  243              None,
  244          )
  245      };
  246      if !session.startup_hints.is_subagent && liveness_watchers_enabled {
  247          let (event_tx, event_rx) =
  248              tokio::sync::mpsc::unbounded_channel::<xai_grok_mcp::servers::McpClientEvent>();
  249          {
  250              let mut mcp_state = session.mcp_state.lock().await;
  251              mcp_state.set_client_event_tx(Some(event_tx));
  252          }
  253          let dispatcher_session_id = session.session_info.id.0.to_string();
  254          let dispatcher_cwd = std::path::PathBuf::from(session.session_info.cwd.as_str());
03
DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

模型与上下文

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factmodelContext

01 · 压缩是一条带预热、两阶段和恢复梯子的子系统

源码事实

会话在阈值前若干百分点预先生成 pass-1；真正压缩支持 two-pass/full-replace，输入溢出时逐级缩小 verbatim/normal conversation，记录多类拒绝原因，并在完成后自动继续。

白话解释

不是等窗口爆了才临时总结：它会提前准备摘要草稿，到红线时再完成第二遍；若摘要输入也太大，就逐级减料。

对自研 Harness 的含义

长任务连续性被当作独立可靠性系统，而非一个 prompt helper。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 3–18
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 19–35
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   35  /// Default percentage points below the auto-compact threshold at which prefire
   36  /// (background pass-1) starts, giving pass-1 runway to finish before the limit.
   37  /// Override with `GROK_PREFIRE_LEAD_PERCENT`.
   38  const DEFAULT_PREFIRE_LEAD_PERCENT: u64 = 10;
   39  fn prefire_lead_percent() -> u64 {
   40      std::env::var("GROK_PREFIRE_LEAD_PERCENT")
   41          .ok()
   42          .and_then(|v| v.trim().parse::<u64>().ok())
   43          .unwrap_or(DEFAULT_PREFIRE_LEAD_PERCENT)
   44  }
   45  /// Cheap fingerprint of a conversation prefix for prefire NOTE₁ validity. A
   46  /// mismatch means the prefix changed (edit / rewind / branch) since pass-1, so
   47  /// the cached NOTE₁ no longer summarizes the current prefix and must be dropped.
   48  fn fingerprint_prefix(items: &[ConversationItem]) -> u64 {
   49      use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
   50      let mut h = std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher::new();
   51      items.len().hash(&mut h);
   52      for it in items {
   53          let tag: u8 = match it {
   54              ConversationItem::System(_) => 0,
   55              ConversationItem::User(_) => 1,
   56              ConversationItem::Assistant(_) => 2,
   57              ConversationItem::ToolResult(_) => 3,
   58              ConversationItem::BackendToolCall(_) => 4,
   59              ConversationItem::Reasoning(_) => 5,
   60          };
   61          tag.hash(&mut h);
   62          it.text_content().hash(&mut h);
   63      }
   64      h.finish()
   65  }
