   24      TWO_PASS_DEFAULT_SPLIT_FRACTION, build_two_pass_pass1_history, build_two_pass_pass2_history,
   25      note_for_two_pass_pass2, split_conversation_for_two_pass,
   26  };
   27  use agent_client_protocol as acp;
   28  use std::sync::Arc;
   29  use xai_chat_state::compaction_utils::{
   30      CompactedHistoryInput, CompactionAttempt, build_compacted_history, is_degenerate_summary,
   31      prepare_conversation_for_verbatim_summarization, sanitize_compacted_history,
   32      validate_compacted_history,
   33  };
   34  use xai_grok_sampling_types::{ApiBackend, ConversationItem};
   35  /// Default percentage points below the auto-compact threshold at which prefire
   36  /// (background pass-1) starts, giving pass-1 runway to finish before the limit.
   37  /// Override with `GROK_PREFIRE_LEAD_PERCENT`.
   38  const DEFAULT_PREFIRE_LEAD_PERCENT: u64 = 10;
   39  fn prefire_lead_percent() -> u64 {
   40      std::env::var("GROK_PREFIRE_LEAD_PERCENT")
   41          .ok()
   42          .and_then(|v| v.trim().parse::<u64>().ok())
   43          .unwrap_or(DEFAULT_PREFIRE_LEAD_PERCENT)
   44  }
   45  /// Cheap fingerprint of a conversation prefix for prefire NOTE₁ validity. A
   46  /// mismatch means the prefix changed (edit / rewind / branch) since pass-1, so
   47  /// the cached NOTE₁ no longer summarizes the current prefix and must be dropped.
   48  fn fingerprint_prefix(items: &[ConversationItem]) -> u64 {
   49      use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
   50      let mut h = std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher::new();
   51      items.len().hash(&mut h);
   52      for it in items {
   53          let tag: u8 = match it {
   54              ConversationItem::System(_) => 0,
   55              ConversationItem::User(_) => 1,
   56              ConversationItem::Assistant(_) => 2,
   57              ConversationItem::ToolResult(_) => 3,
   58              ConversationItem::BackendToolCall(_) => 4,
   59              ConversationItem::Reasoning(_) => 5,
   60          };
   61          tag.hash(&mut h);
   62          it.text_content().hash(&mut h);
   63      }
   64      h.finish()
   65  }
   66  /// Outcome of a background prefire pass-1 run, recorded on the
   67  /// `session.prefire_pass1` span as `compaction_prefire_outcome`.
   68  /// [`PrefireOutcome::as_str`] values are stable telemetry keys
   69  /// (telemetry/dashboards key off them) — don't rename the strings.
   70  #[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Debug)]
   71  enum PrefireOutcome {
   72      Cached,
   73      Disabled,
   74      DebugFailPass1,
   75      TooSmall,
   76      EmptySplit,
   77      SampleFailed,
   78      EmptyNote1,
   79  }
   80  impl PrefireOutcome {
   81      fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
   82          match self {
   83              Self::Cached => "cached",
   84              Self::Disabled => "disabled",
   85              Self::DebugFailPass1 => "debug_fail_pass1",
   86              Self::TooSmall => "too_small",
   87              Self::EmptySplit => "empty_split",
   88              Self::SampleFailed => "sample_failed",
   89              Self::EmptyNote1 => "empty_note1",
   90          }
   91      }
   92  }
   93  /// Telemetry from one prefire pass-1 run; recorded onto the
   94  /// `session.prefire_pass1` span by [`SessionActor::run_prefire_pass1`].
   95  /// `None` fields = the run exited before that stage.
   96  struct PrefirePass1Run {
   97      outcome: PrefireOutcome,
   98      prefix_len: Option<usize>,
   99      prefix_est_tokens: Option<u64>,
  100      pass1_latency_ms: Option<u64>,
  101      note1_chars: Option<usize>,
  102  }
  103  impl From<PrefireOutcome> for PrefirePass1Run {
  104      /// A run that exited before splitting/sampling — outcome only.
  105      fn from(outcome: PrefireOutcome) -> Self {
  106          Self {
02
L1factmodelContext

02 · 压缩后重建的是“任务状态”，不是纯聊天摘要

源码事实

压缩会把 AGENTS.md、skills、编辑路径、运行中子 Agent、MCP server、Todo、Plan mode 和 memory 状态重新注入；清理孤儿 ToolResult，验证失败则退到无 recent_messages 的最小历史，并持久化 segment/checkpoint。

白话解释

总结完以后还会把“哪些子任务在跑、待办是什么、插件有哪些、当前计划阶段”重新装回去，避免只剩一段模糊回忆。

对自研 Harness 的含义

可靠 compaction 必须有结构化状态源，不能只依赖模型自由文本。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1282–1370
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1371–1460
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

 1390                                      })
 1391                                      .collect()
 1392                              })
 1393                              .unwrap_or_default()
 1394                      };
 1395                      CompactionStateContext::build(
 1396                          &conversation,
 1397                          CompactionInputs {
 1398                              running_tasks,
 1399                              running_subagents,
 1400                              agent_edited_paths: edited_paths.clone(),
 1401                              connected_mcp_servers,
 1402                              todos,
 1403                              ..Default::default()
 1404                          },
 1405                      )
 1406                      .await
 1407                  };
 1408                  (agents_md, skills, edited_paths, ctx)
 1409              };
 1410          use crate::session::helpers::compaction_context::SubagentToolNames;
 1411          let subagent_tool_names: Option<SubagentToolNames> =
 1412              if use_short_prompt || state_context.running_subagents.is_empty() {
 1413                  None
 1414              } else {
 1415                  let agent_ref = self.agent.borrow();
 1416                  let bridge = agent_ref.tool_bridge();
 1417                  let empty = serde_json::json!({});
 1418                  let poll_name = bridge
 1419                      .render_prompt("${{ tools.by_kind.background_task_action }}", &empty)
 1420                      .await
 1421                      .filter(|s| !s.is_empty() && !s.contains("by_kind"));
 1422                  let cancel_name = bridge
 1423                      .render_prompt("${{ tools.by_kind.kill_task_action }}", &empty)
 1424                      .await
 1425                      .filter(|s| !s.is_empty() && !s.contains("by_kind"));
 1426                  match (poll_name, cancel_name) {
 1427                      (Some(poll), Some(cancel)) => Some(SubagentToolNames { poll, cancel }),
 1428                      (poll, cancel) => {
 1429                          tracing::warn!(
 1430                              session_id = %self.session_info.id.0,
 1431                              poll_resolved = poll.is_some(),
 1432                              cancel_resolved = cancel.is_some(),
 1433                              "could not resolve subagent tool names, \
 1434                               omitting subagent reminder from compacted conversation"
 1435                          );
 1436                          None
 1437                      }
 1438                  }
 1439              };
 1440          use crate::session::helpers::compaction_context::McpToolNames;
 1441          let mcp_tool_names: Option<McpToolNames> =
 1442              if use_short_prompt || state_context.connected_mcp_servers.is_empty() {
 1443                  None
 1444              } else {
 1445                  let agent_ref = self.agent.borrow();
 1446                  let bridge = agent_ref.tool_bridge();
 1447                  let empty = serde_json::json!({});
 1448                  let search_name = bridge
 1449                      .render_prompt("${{ tools.by_kind.search_tool }}", &empty)
 1450                      .await
 1451                      .filter(|s| !s.is_empty() && !s.contains("by_kind"));
 1452                  let call_name = bridge
 1453                      .render_prompt("${{ tools.by_kind.use_tool }}", &empty)
 1454                      .await
 1455                      .filter(|s| !s.is_empty() && !s.contains("by_kind"));
 1456                  match (search_name, call_name) {
 1457                      (Some(search), Some(call)) => Some(McpToolNames { search, call }),
 1458                      _ => None,
 1459                  }
 1460              };
 1461          let memory_backend_impl = {
 1462              let g = self.memory.storage.borrow();
 1463              g.as_ref()
 1464                  .zip(self.memory.backend_params.as_ref())
 1465                  .map(|(storage, params)| {
 1466                      crate::session::memory::MemoryBackendImpl::from_session_params(
 1467                          storage.clone(),
 1468                          &crate::session::memory::MemoryBackendParams {
 1469                              search_source: "compaction_recovery",
 1470                              ..params.clone()
 1471                          },
 1472                      )
 1473                  })
 1474          };
 1475          let memory_opt_out = false;
 1476          let memory_ref: Option<&dyn xai_grok_tools::types::memory_backend::MemoryBackend> =
 1477              if memory_opt_out {
 1478                  None
 1479              } else {
 1480                  memory_backend_impl
 1481                      .as_ref()
 1482                      .map(|b| b as &dyn xai_grok_tools::types::memory_backend::MemoryBackend)
 1483              };
 1484          let suppress_state_reminder = false;
 1485          let system_reminder = if suppress_state_reminder {
 1486              None
 1487          } else {
 1488              to_system_reminder(
 1489                  &state_context,
 1490                  &discovered_agents_md,
 1491                  &all_skills_for_compaction,
 1492                  memory_ref,
 1493                  subagent_tool_names.as_ref(),
 1494                  mcp_tool_names.as_ref(),
 1495              )
 1496              .await
 1497          };
 1498          let system_reminder = {
 1499              let plan_path = {
 1500                  let guard = self.plan_mode.lock();
 1501                  guard
 1502                      .is_active()
 1503                      .then(|| guard.plan_file_path().to_path_buf())
 1504              };
 1505              if let Some(plan_path) = plan_path {
 1506                  let plan_has_content =
 1507                      crate::session::plan_mode::plan_file_has_content(&plan_path).await;
 1508                  let template = crate::session::plan_mode::plan_mode_reminder_full_template();
 1509                  let wrapper = self.reminder_wrapper_tag();
 1510                  let rendered = self
 1511                      .render_plan_template(template, &plan_path, plan_has_content)
 1512                      .await;
 1513                  match (system_reminder, rendered) {
 1514                      (Some(mut existing), Some(plan_section)) => {
 1515                          if let Some(pos) = existing.rfind("</system-reminder>") {
 1516                              existing.insert_str(pos, &format!("\n\n{}\n", plan_section));
 1517                          } else {
 1518                              existing.push_str("\n\n");
 1519                              existing.push_str(&plan_section);
 1520                          }
 1521                          Some(existing)
 1522                      }
 1523                      (None, Some(plan_section)) => Some(format!(
 1524                          "<{tag}>\n{body}\n</{tag}>",
 1525                          tag = wrapper,
 1526                          body = plan_section,
 1527                      )),
 1528                      (existing, None) => {
 1529                          tracing::warn!(
 1530                              session_id = %self.session_info.id.0,
 1531                              "compaction: plan mode active but template render failed"
04
DIMENSION · EXECUTION

执行环境与沙箱

4 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factexecution

01 · 提供真正的内核级文件系统沙箱

源码事实

默认 enforce feature 在 Unix 使用 nono，映射到 Landlock/Seatbelt；Profile capability set 控制全盘读、显式只读/读写、deny，应用后不可逆并覆盖进程内文件 API 与子进程。

白话解释

这不只是“执行前问一下”，操作系统内核会真的挡住不允许的文件访问。

对自研 Harness 的含义

在这 14 个项目里，Grok Build 的安全边界属于更强的系统级设计。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 8–12
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 13–18
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

    8  //! OS-level sandboxing for Grok Build via [nono](https://crates.io/crates/nono).
    9  //!
   10  //! Applied once at process startup. Covers in-process `tokio::fs` calls
   11  //! and child processes. Network is left open at the process level (agent
   12  //! needs LLM API); child network is blocked per-subprocess via seccomp.
   13  //!
   14  //! The `enforce` feature (on by default) pulls in `nono` for
   15  //! kernel-enforced sandboxing (Landlock/Seatbelt). When disabled, the
   16  //! crate still provides lightweight helpers (`log_violation`,
   17  //! `should_restrict_child_network`, `child_net`) that compile on all
   18  //! targets including musl.
   19  //!

   21  //! use xai_grok_sandbox::{SandboxManager, ProfileName};
   22  //! use std::path::Path;
   23  //!
   24  //! let workspace = Path::new("/home/user/project");
   25  //! let mut sandbox = SandboxManager::new(ProfileName::Workspace, workspace);
   26  //! sandbox.apply(workspace).expect("sandbox apply failed");
   27  //! sandbox.install();
   28  //! ```

   48  /// enforcing profiles). Shell fails closed when protection cannot be applied.
   49  pub fn requires_hook_write_deny(profile: &ProfileName, workspace: &Path) -> bool {
   50      if !profile_enforces_hook_write_deny(profile) || *profile == ProfileName::Off {
   51          return false;
   52      }
   53      let config = profiles::load_sandbox_config(workspace);
   54      match profile {
   55          ProfileName::Custom(name) => {
   56              config.profiles.get(name).and_then(|p| p.extends.as_deref()) != Some("devbox")
   57          }
   58          ProfileName::Devbox => false,
   59          _ => true,
   60      }
   61  }

02
L1limitationexecution

02 · 沙箱不支持或应用失败时会降级继续

源码事实

若平台不支持、apply 失败或构建关闭 enforce feature，SandboxManager 记录警告和 apply_failed 事件后返回 Ok，进程继续运行；is_active 可用于区分实际生效与仅请求。

白话解释

配置了沙箱不等于一定锁住；系统不支持时默认是“报警后继续”，不是整个 Agent 拒绝启动。

对自研 Harness 的含义

企业部署必须把“sandbox requested”和“sandbox active”分开做启动门禁；需要 fail-closed 时应由外层策略强制。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 107–117
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 118–129
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 02d9359435d0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
crates/codegen/xai-grok-sandbox/src/lib.rs:107–129固定提交 ↗
  107  /// The non-`off` sandbox profile this process was **requested** with, if any.
  108  ///
  109  /// This is the configured request, not a report that enforcement succeeded —
  110  /// `is_active()` can be false while the process is still confined (e.g. some
  111  /// Linux bwrap paths), and a requested-but-unapplied profile already warns the
  112  /// user. Keying on the request is the fail-closed choice.
  113  pub fn requested_confinement_profile() -> Option<&'static str> {
  114      configured_profile_name().filter(|name| profile_confines(name))
  115  }
  116  fn profile_confines(name: &str) -> bool {
  117      name.parse::<ProfileName>()
  118          .is_ok_and(|profile| profile != ProfileName::Off)
  119  }
  120  /// Whether the sandbox was successfully applied to this process.
  121  pub fn is_active() -> bool {
  122      SANDBOX.get().is_some_and(|s| s.applied)
  123  }
  124  /// The active sandbox profile name, or `None` if sandbox is not applied.
  125  pub fn profile_name() -> Option<&'static str> {
  126      SANDBOX
  127          .get()
  128          .filter(|s| s.applied)
  129          .map(|s| s.profile.as_str())
  130  }
  131  /// Log a sandbox violation. Immediately flushed to disk.
  132  /// No-op if sandbox is not active.
  133  pub fn log_violation(target: &str, operation: &str) {
  134      if let Some(state) = SANDBOX.get() {
  135          state.logger.log(SandboxEvent::fs_violation(
  136              &state.profile,
  137              target,
  138              operation,
  139          ));
  140          let _ = state.logger.flush_to_disk();
  141      }
  142  }
  143  /// Flush sandbox events to disk. No-op if not initialized.
  144  pub fn flush() {
  145      if let Some(state) = SANDBOX.get()
  146          && let Err(e) = state.logger.flush_to_disk()
  147      {
  148          tracing::warn!(error = %e, "Failed to flush sandbox events to disk");
  149      }
  150  }
  151  /// Violation metrics, or `None` if sandbox is not active.
  152  pub fn metrics() -> Option<&'static SandboxMetrics> {
  153      SANDBOX.get().map(|s| s.logger.metrics())
  154  }
  155  /// Manages the OS-level sandbox. Call `apply()` then `install()`.
  156  pub struct SandboxManager {
  157      profile: ProfileName,
  158      logger: SandboxLogger,
  159      net_restricted: bool,
  160      applied: bool,
  161  }
  162  impl SandboxManager {
  163      /// Create a sandbox manager. Does not apply until `apply()` is called.
  164      pub fn new(profile: ProfileName, _workspace: &Path) -> Self {
  165          let net_restricted = profile.restricts_network();
  166          Self {
  167              profile,
  168              logger: SandboxLogger::new(),
  169              net_restricted,
  170              applied: false,
  171          }
  172      }
  173      /// Apply the sandbox to the current process. **Irreversible.**
  174      /// Degrades gracefully if the platform doesn't support it.
  175      #[cfg(all(feature = "enforce", unix))]
  176      pub fn apply(&mut self, workspace: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
  177          if self.profile == ProfileName::Off {
  178              tracing::info!("Sandbox disabled (profile: off)");
  179              return Ok(());
  180          }
  181          if requires_hook_write_deny(&self.profile, workspace) {
  182              xai_grok_config::ensure_grok_hook_slots(paths::grok_home().as_path())
  183                  .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("hook write-deny ensure failed: {e}"))?;
  184              hook_write_deny::maybe_install_namespace_lockdown_inside_bwrap(&self.profile)
  185                  .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("{e}"))?;
  186          }
  187          let config = profiles::load_sandbox_config(workspace);
  188          let mut resolved = self.profile.resolve_profile(workspace, &config)?;
  189          self.net_restricted = resolved.restrict_network;
  190          let support = Sandbox::support_info();
  191          if !support.is_supported {
  192              tracing::warn!(
  193                  details = %support.details,
  194                  "Sandbox not supported on this platform, continuing without sandbox"
  195              );
  196              self.logger.log(SandboxEvent::apply_failed(
  197                  &self.profile.to_string(),
  198                  workspace,
  199                  &support.details,
  200              ));

  107  pub fn configured_profile_name() -> Option<&'static str> {
  108      CONFIGURED_PROFILE.get().map(|s| s.as_str())

  110  /// The non-`off` sandbox profile this process was **requested** with, if any.
  111  ///
  112  /// This is the configured request, not a report that enforcement succeeded —
  113  /// `is_active()` can be false while the process is still confined (e.g. some
  114  /// Linux bwrap paths), and a requested-but-unapplied profile already warns the
  115  /// user. Keying on the request is the fail-closed choice.
  116  pub fn requested_confinement_profile() -> Option<&'static str> {
  117      configured_profile_name().filter(|name| profile_confines(name))
  118  }
  119  fn profile_confines(name: &str) -> bool {
  120      name.parse::<ProfileName>()
  121          .is_ok_and(|profile| profile != ProfileName::Off)
  122  }
  123  /// Whether the sandbox was successfully applied to this process.
  124  pub fn is_active() -> bool {
  125      SANDBOX.get().is_some_and(|s| s.applied)
  126  }
  127  /// The active sandbox profile name, or `None` if sandbox is not applied.
  128  pub fn profile_name() -> Option<&'static str> {
  129      SANDBOX
  130          .get()
  131          .filter(|s| s.applied)
  132          .map(|s| s.profile.as_str())
  133  }
  134  /// Log a sandbox violation. Immediately flushed to disk.
  135  /// No-op if sandbox is not active.
  136  pub fn log_violation(target: &str, operation: &str) {
  137      if let Some(state) = SANDBOX.get() {
  138          state.logger.log(SandboxEvent::fs_violation(
  139              &state.profile,
  140              target,
  141              operation,
  142          ));
  143          let _ = state.logger.flush_to_disk();
  144      }
  145  }
  146  /// Flush sandbox events to disk. No-op if not initialized.
  147  pub fn flush() {
  148      if let Some(state) = SANDBOX.get()
  149          && let Err(e) = state.logger.flush_to_disk()
  150      {
  151          tracing::warn!(error = %e, "Failed to flush sandbox events to disk");
  152      }
  153  }
  154  /// Violation metrics, or `None` if sandbox is not active.

  156      SANDBOX.get().map(|s| s.logger.metrics())
  157  }

  161      logger: SandboxLogger,
  162      net_restricted: bool,
  163      applied: bool,
  164  }
  165  impl SandboxManager {
  166      /// Create a sandbox manager. Does not apply until `apply()` is called.
  167      pub fn new(profile: ProfileName, _workspace: &Path) -> Self {
  168          let net_restricted = profile.restricts_network();
  169          Self {
  170              profile,
  171              logger: SandboxLogger::new(),
  172              net_restricted,
  173              applied: false,
  174          }
  175      }
  176      /// Apply the sandbox to the current process. **Irreversible.**
  177      /// Degrades gracefully if the platform doesn't support it.
  178      #[cfg(all(feature = "enforce", unix))]
  179      pub fn apply(&mut self, workspace: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
  180          if self.profile == ProfileName::Off {
  181              tracing::info!("Sandbox disabled (profile: off)");
  182              return Ok(());
  183          }
  184          if requires_hook_write_deny(&self.profile, workspace) {
  185              xai_grok_config::ensure_grok_hook_slots(paths::grok_home().as_path())
  186                  .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("hook write-deny ensure failed: {e}"))?;
  187              hook_write_deny::maybe_install_namespace_lockdown_inside_bwrap(&self.profile)
  188                  .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("{e}"))?;
  189          }
  190          let config = profiles::load_sandbox_config(workspace);
  191          let mut resolved = self.profile.resolve_profile(workspace, &config)?;
  192          self.net_restricted = resolved.restrict_network;
  193          let support = Sandbox::support_info();
  194          if !support.is_supported {
  195              tracing::warn!(
  196                  details = %support.details,
  197                  "Sandbox not supported on this platform, continuing without sandbox"
  198              );
  199              self.logger.log(SandboxEvent::apply_failed(
  200                  &self.profile.to_string(),
03
L1factexecution

03 · 子进程网络隔离与主进程网络分离

源码事实

主进程保留网络供 LLM API；ReadOnly/Strict profile 在已生效的 Linux 沙箱中，对已知子进程启动路径安装 seccomp 网络过滤，并额外锁定 namespace 逃逸 syscall。

白话解释

Agent 自己可以连模型服务，但它启动的 bash 不一定能上网，减少 curl 下载执行或数据外传风险。

对自研 Harness 的含义

网络隔离需要按进程角色拆分；完全断主进程网络会破坏云模型调用。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 8–12
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 13–18
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   10  //! Applied once at process startup. Covers in-process `tokio::fs` calls
   11  //! and child processes. Network is left open at the process level (agent
   12  //! needs LLM API); child network is blocked per-subprocess via seccomp.
   13  //!
   14  //! The `enforce` feature (on by default) pulls in `nono` for
   15  //! kernel-enforced sandboxing (Landlock/Seatbelt). When disabled, the
   16  //! crate still provides lightweight helpers (`log_violation`,
   17  //! `should_restrict_child_network`, `child_net`) that compile on all
   18  //! targets including musl.
   19  //!

   22  //! use std::path::Path;
   23  //!
   24  //! let workspace = Path::new("/home/user/project");
   25  //! let mut sandbox = SandboxManager::new(ProfileName::Workspace, workspace);

   27  //! sandbox.install();
   28  //! ```

   47  /// Whether this profile requires direct-hook write protection (non-devbox
   48  /// enforcing profiles). Shell fails closed when protection cannot be applied.
   49  pub fn requires_hook_write_deny(profile: &ProfileName, workspace: &Path) -> bool {
   50      if !profile_enforces_hook_write_deny(profile) || *profile == ProfileName::Off {
   51          return false;
   52      }
   53      let config = profiles::load_sandbox_config(workspace);
   54      match profile {
   55          ProfileName::Custom(name) => {
   56              config.profiles.get(name).and_then(|p| p.extends.as_deref()) != Some("devbox")
   57          }
   58          ProfileName::Devbox => false,
   59          _ => true,
   60      }
   61  }

L1factexecution

04 · 项目不能覆写同名全局安全 Profile

源码事实

全局 ~/.grok/sandbox.toml 先加载；项目 .grok/sandbox.toml 只能新增自定义 profile，若名称已存在则忽略项目定义。

白话解释

恶意仓库不能悄悄做一个同名“strict”配置，把你信任的全局规则掏空。

对自研 Harness 的含义

配置合并本身也是信任边界，不能简单 last-write-wins。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 113–139
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 140–167
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 02d9359435d0 · 打开账本提交 ↗
crates/codegen/xai-grok-sandbox/src/profiles.rs:113–167固定提交 ↗
  113  /// Load sandbox config from `~/.grok/sandbox.toml` and `.grok/sandbox.toml`.
  114  ///
  115  /// Project config may **add** new profile names only. It cannot redefine a
  116  /// name already present in the global config — last-write-wins would let a
  117  /// malicious workspace hollow out a user/enterprise custom profile (e.g.
  118  /// empty `deny` / broad `read_write`) while keeping the trusted name.
  119  pub fn load_sandbox_config(workspace: &Path) -> SandboxConfig {
  120      let mut config = SandboxConfig::default();
  121
  122      // Global config: ~/.grok/sandbox.toml
  123      let global_path = grok_home().join("sandbox.toml");
  124      if let Some(global) = load_config_file(&global_path) {
  125          config = global;
  126      }
  127
  128      // Project config: <workspace>/.grok/sandbox.toml (additive only)
  129      let project_path = workspace.join(".grok").join("sandbox.toml");
  130      if let Some(project) = load_config_file(&project_path) {
  131          merge_project_profiles(&mut config, project);
  132      }
  133
  134      config
  135  }
  136
  137  pub fn sandbox_profile_conflicts(workspace: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
  138      let global = load_config_file(&grok_home().join("sandbox.toml")).unwrap_or_default();
  139      let project =
  140          load_config_file(&workspace.join(".grok").join("sandbox.toml")).unwrap_or_default();
  141      mismatched_profile_names(&global, &project)
  142  }
  143
  144  fn mismatched_profile_names(global: &SandboxConfig, project: &SandboxConfig) -> Vec<String> {
  145      let mut names: Vec<String> = project
  146          .profiles
  147          .iter()
  148          .filter(|(name, _)| matches!(name.parse(), Ok(ProfileName::Custom(_))))
  149          .filter_map(|(name, project_profile)| {
  150              global
  151                  .profiles
  152                  .get(name)
  153                  .filter(|global_profile| *global_profile != project_profile)
  154                  .map(|_| name.to_owned())
  155          })
  156          .collect();
  157      names.sort_unstable();
  158      names
  159  }
  160
  161  /// Merge project profiles into `config`. Names already defined globally are
  162  /// ignored so a workspace cannot replace a global custom profile's policy.
  163  fn merge_project_profiles(config: &mut SandboxConfig, project: SandboxConfig) {
  164      for (name, profile) in project.profiles {
  165          config.profiles.entry(name).or_insert(profile);
  166      }
  167  }
  168
  169  fn load_config_file(path: &Path) -> Option<SandboxConfig> {
  170      let content = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok()?;
  171      match toml::from_str(&content) {
  172          Ok(config) => Some(config),
  173          Err(e) => {
  174              tracing::warn!(path = %path.display(), error = %e, "Failed to parse sandbox config");
  175              None
  176          }
  177      }
  178  }
  179
  180  /// Whether a device **file** entry is safe to pass to `allow_file` / Landlock
  181  /// PathFd materialization.
  182  ///
  183  /// `/dev/tty` always exists, but without a controlling terminal `open()` returns
  184  /// ENXIO and nono's apply aborts the **entire** ruleset. Built-in profiles fail
  185  /// open, which was a silent sandbox bypass under `setsid`/CI/headless launches.
  186  ///
  187  /// Only that class of failure (and missing nodes) is filtered here. Other open
  188  /// errors — notably **EISDIR** on directory nodes — must not drop the path:
  189  /// directories are granted via [`DEVICE_DIRS`] / `allow_path`, and a plain
  190  /// `File::open` EISDIR does not mean Landlock would reject the grant.
  191  #[cfg(all(feature = "enforce", unix))]
  192  fn device_file_openable(path: &Path) -> bool {
  193      match std::fs::File::open(path) {
  194          Ok(_) => true,
  195          Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => false,
  196          // ENXIO/ENODEV: e.g. /dev/tty with no controlling terminal — PathFd
  197          // materialization would abort the whole Landlock ruleset.
  198          Err(e) if matches!(e.raw_os_error(), Some(libc::ENXIO) | Some(libc::ENODEV)) => false,
  199          // EISDIR, EACCES, etc.: still attempt the grant path. allow_file may
  200          // reject ExpectedFile; that only skips this entry, not the whole apply.
  201          Err(_) => true,
  202      }
  203  }
  204
  205  impl ProfileName {
  206      /// Convert this profile into a nono `CapabilitySet` for the given workspace.
  207      #[cfg(all(feature = "enforce", unix))]
  208      pub fn to_capability_set(&self, workspace: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<CapabilitySet> {
  209          let config = load_sandbox_config(workspace);
  210          self.to_capability_set_with_config(workspace, &config)
  211      }
  212
  213      /// Convert using an already-loaded config (avoids re-reading disk).
  214      ///
  215      /// A custom profile's own `deny` list is kernel-enforced (read + write/rename)
  216      /// on top of the base profile.
  217      #[cfg(all(feature = "enforce", unix))]
  218      pub fn to_capability_set_with_config(
  219          &self,
  220          workspace: &Path,
  221          config: &SandboxConfig,
  222      ) -> anyhow::Result<CapabilitySet> {
  223          if *self == Self::Off {
  224              return Ok(CapabilitySet::new());
  225          }
  226
  227          let profile = self.resolve_profile(workspace, config)?;
  228          Self::capability_set_from_profile(workspace, &profile)
  229      }
  230
  231      #[cfg(all(feature = "enforce", unix))]
  232      pub(crate) fn capability_set_from_profile(
  233          workspace: &Path,
  234          profile: &SandboxProfile,
  235      ) -> anyhow::Result<CapabilitySet> {
  236          let mut caps = CapabilitySet::new();
  237
  238          // Default read access

crates/codegen/xai-grok-sandbox/src/profiles.rs · e5fd4816d432当前源码 ↗
  113
  114  /// Load sandbox config from `~/.grok/sandbox.toml` and `.grok/sandbox.toml`.
  115  ///
  116  /// Project config may **add** new profile names only. It cannot redefine a
  117  /// name already present in the global config — last-write-wins would let a
  118  /// malicious workspace hollow out a user/enterprise custom profile (e.g.
  119  /// empty `deny` / broad `read_write`) while keeping the trusted name.
  120  pub fn load_sandbox_config(workspace: &Path) -> SandboxConfig {
  121      let mut config = SandboxConfig::default();
  122
  123      // Global config: ~/.grok/sandbox.toml
  124      let global_path = grok_home().join("sandbox.toml");
  125      if let Some(global) = load_config_file(&global_path) {
  126          config = global;
  127      }
  128
  129      // Project config: <workspace>/.grok/sandbox.toml (additive only)
  130      let project_path = workspace.join(".grok").join("sandbox.toml");
  131      if let Some(project) = load_config_file(&project_path) {
  132          merge_project_profiles(&mut config, project);
  133      }
  134
  135      config
  136  }
  137
  138  pub fn sandbox_profile_conflicts(workspace: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
  139      let global = load_config_file(&grok_home().join("sandbox.toml")).unwrap_or_default();
  140      let project =
  141          load_config_file(&workspace.join(".grok").join("sandbox.toml")).unwrap_or_default();
  142      mismatched_profile_names(&global, &project)
  143  }
  144
  145  fn mismatched_profile_names(global: &SandboxConfig, project: &SandboxConfig) -> Vec<String> {
  146      let mut names: Vec<String> = project
  147          .profiles
  148          .iter()
  149          .filter(|(name, _)| matches!(name.parse(), Ok(ProfileName::Custom(_))))
  150          .filter_map(|(name, project_profile)| {
  151              global
  152                  .profiles
  153                  .get(name)
  154                  .filter(|global_profile| *global_profile != project_profile)
  155                  .map(|_| name.to_owned())
  156          })
  157          .collect();
  158      names.sort_unstable();
  159      names
  160  }
  161
  162  /// Merge project profiles into `config`. Names already defined globally are
  163  /// ignored so a workspace cannot replace a global custom profile's policy.
  164  fn merge_project_profiles(config: &mut SandboxConfig, project: SandboxConfig) {
  165      for (name, profile) in project.profiles {
  166          config.profiles.entry(name).or_insert(profile);
  167      }
  168  }
  169
  170  fn load_config_file(path: &Path) -> Option<SandboxConfig> {
  171      let content = std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok()?;
  172      match toml::from_str(&content) {
  173          Ok(config) => Some(config),
  174          Err(e) => {
  175              tracing::warn!(path = %path.display(), error = %e, "Failed to parse sandbox config");
  176              None
  177          }
  178      }
  179  }

05
DIMENSION · TOOLS

工具注册与执行

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1facttools

01 · 工具执行明确拆成 prepare、并发 dispatch、post-flight

源码事实

execute_tool_calls_batch 先逐个解析、运行 Plan gate、hooks 和权限；批准后用 FuturesUnordered 并发执行，写同一路径的工具共享 Mutex 串行；完成后按原 slot 做事件和结果处理。

白话解释

能并行的尽量并行，但两个工具若同时写同一个文件会排队，避免互相覆盖。

对自研 Harness 的含义

并发策略不是简单 all-at-once，而是带资源锁的调度。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 355–401
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 402–449
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  355      /// Prepare → dispatch → post-flight. Caller owns the outer tail flush.
  356      async fn execute_tool_calls_batch(
  357          &self,
  358          tool_calls: Vec<crate::sampling::types::ToolCallResponse>,
  359          deferred_followups: &mut Vec<ConversationItem>,
  360          final_result: &mut Option<ToolLoop>,
  361      ) -> Result<(), acp::Error> {
  362          let mut approved: Vec<PreparedToolCall> = Vec::new();
  363          for call in tool_calls.into_iter() {
  364              if final_result.is_some() {
  365                  let message = match &*final_result {
  366                      Some(ToolLoop::PermissionReject { .. }) => {
  367                          format!(
  368                              "Tool execution cancelled due to earlier permission rejection for tool `{}`",
  369                              call.function.name
  370                          )
  371                      }
  372                      Some(ToolLoop::Cancelled) => {
  373                          format!(
  374                              "Tool execution cancelled due to earlier user cancellation for tool `{}`",
  375                              call.function.name
  376                          )
  377                      }
  378                      Some(ToolLoop::FollowupMessage(_)) => {
  379                          format!(
  380                              "Tool execution cancelled due to earlier user followup message for tool `{}`",
  381                              call.function.name
  382                          )
  383                      }
  384                      _ => {
  385                          format!("Tool execution cancelled for tool `{}`", call.function.name)
  386                      }
  387                  };
  388                  self.chat_state_handle
  389                      .push_tool_result(ConversationItem::tool_result(call.id.clone(), message));
  390                  continue;
  391              }
  392              self.emit_event(crate::session::events::Event::ToolStarted {
  393                  tool_name: call.function.name.clone(),
  394              });
  395              self.observability_bridge
  396                  .emit(
  397                      xai_tool_protocol::session_event::SessionEvent::ToolCallStarted {
  398                          tool_call_id: call.id.clone(),
  399                          tool_name: call.function.name.clone(),
  400                          turn_number: self.current_turn_number.get(),
  401                      },
  402                  )
  403                  .await;
  404              let call_name = call.function.name.clone();
  405              match self.prepare_tool_call(call, deferred_followups).await? {
  406                  Ok(prepared) => approved.push(prepared),
  407                  Err(tool_loop) => {
  408                      self.events.tool_finished();
  409                      if let Some((server, tool)) =
  410                          crate::session::mcp_servers::parse_mcp_tool_name(&call_name)
  411                      {
  412                          let error_reason = match &tool_loop {
  413                              ToolLoop::PermissionReject { reason, .. } => reason.clone(),
  414                              ToolLoop::Cancelled => "cancelled".to_string(),
  415                              ToolLoop::FollowupMessage(_) => "followup".to_string(),
  416                              ToolLoop::HookDenied { hook_name, .. } => {
  417                                  format!("hook_denied:{hook_name}")
  418                              }
  419                              other => format!("{other:?}"),
  420                          };
  421                          self.emit_event(xai_file_utils::events::Event::McpToolCallCompleted {
  422                              server_name: server.to_string(),
  423                              tool_name: tool.to_string(),
  424                              call_id: format!(
  425                                  "{}{}{}",
  426                                  server,
  427                                  crate::session::mcp_servers::MCP_TOOL_NAME_DELIMITER,
  428                                  tool
  429                              ),
  430                              duration_ms: 0,
  431                              success: false,
  432                              is_timeout: false,
  433                              error: Some(error_reason),
  434                              reconnect_attempted: false,
  435                              auth_retry_attempted: false,
  436                          });
  437                      }
  438                      if matches!(
  439                          tool_loop,
  440                          ToolLoop::PermissionReject { .. }
  441                              | ToolLoop::Cancelled
  442                              | ToolLoop::FollowupMessage(_)
  443                      ) && final_result.is_none()
  444                      {
  445                          *final_result = Some(tool_loop);
  446                      }
  447                  }
  448              }
  449          }
  450          if approved.iter().any(|p| p.tool_name == "search_tool") {
  451              self.retry_auth_required_servers().await;
  452          }
  453          let write_paths: std::collections::HashSet<String> = approved
  454              .iter()
  455              .filter(|prepared| !prepared.is_read_only)
  456              .filter_map(|prepared| lock_path_for_args(&prepared.parsed_args).map(str::to_owned))
  457              .collect();
  458          let file_locks = {
  459              let mut map: std::collections::HashMap<String, Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<()>>> =
  460                  std::collections::HashMap::new();
  461              for prepared in &approved {
  462                  if let Some(fp) = lock_path_for_args(&prepared.parsed_args)
  463                      && write_paths.contains(fp)
  464                  {
  465                      map.entry(fp.to_owned())
  466                          .or_insert_with(|| Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(())));
  467                  }
  468              }
  469              map
  470          };
  471          let shared_recovery = Arc::new(tokio::sync::OnceCell::<bool>::const_new());
  472          let workspace_ops = self.workspace_ops.clone();
  473          let pending_interjections = self.pending_interjections.clone();
  474          let session_id: Arc<str> = Arc::from(&*self.session_info.id.0);
  475          let dispatch_futures: Vec<_> = approved
  476              .iter()
  477              .enumerate()
  478              .map(|(idx, prepared)| {
  479                  let prepared = Arc::new(prepared.clone());
  480                  let am = self.auth_manager.clone();
  481                  let shared_recovery = Arc::clone(&shared_recovery);
  482                  let workspace_ops = workspace_ops.clone();
  483                  let session_id = session_id.clone();
  484                  let pending_interjections = pending_interjections.clone();
  485                  let blocking_wait_depth = self.tool_context.blocking_wait_depth.clone();
  486                  let interruptible =
  487                      is_interruptible_wait_tool(&prepared.tool_name, &prepared.parsed_args);
  488                  let lock = lock_path_for_args(&prepared.parsed_args)
  489                      .and_then(|fp| file_locks.get(fp).cloned());
  490                  async move {
  491                      let exec_start = std::time::Instant::now();
  492                      let run_tool = || {
  493                          let prepared = Arc::clone(&prepared);
  494                          let workspace_ops = workspace_ops.clone();
  495                          let session_id = session_id.clone();
  496                          let lock = lock.clone();
  497                          async move {
  498                              let _guard = if let Some(ref l) = lock {
  499                                  Some(l.lock().await)
  500                              } else {
  501                                  None
  502                              };
  503                              dispatch_tool(&workspace_ops, &prepared, &session_id).await
  504                          }
  505                      };
  506                      let result = if interruptible {
  507                          let _wait_guard = BlockingWaitGuard::enter(blocking_wait_depth.clone());
  508                          tokio::select! {
  509                              biased;
  510                              result = call_with_auth_retry(
  511                                  am.as_ref(),
  512                                  Some(&shared_recovery),
  513                                  &prepared.tool_name,
  514                                  run_tool,
  515                              ) => result,
  516                              _ = wait_for_pending_interjection(&pending_interjections) => {
  517                                  tracing::info!(
  518                                      tool = %prepared.tool_name,
  519                                      "abort wait tool: interjection pending"
  520                                  );

  355      pub(super) async fn execute_tool_calls(
  356          &self,
  357          tool_calls: Vec<crate::sampling::types::ToolCallResponse>,
  358      ) -> Result<ToolLoop, acp::Error> {
  359          if let Some(cfg) = self.chat_state_handle.get_sampling_config().await {
  360              tracing::Span::current().record("model_id", cfg.model.as_str());
  361          }
  362          let mut final_result: Option<ToolLoop> = None;
  363          let mut deferred_followups: Vec<ConversationItem> = Vec::new();
  364          if tool_calls.len() > 1 {
  365              let kind_of = |name: &str| self.agent.borrow().tool_bridge().tool_kind(name);
  366              let (body, tail) = split_exit_plan_tail(tool_calls, kind_of);
  367              if !body.is_empty() {
  368                  self.execute_tool_calls_batch(body, &mut deferred_followups, &mut final_result)
  369                      .await?;
  370              }
  371              if !tail.is_empty() {
  372                  self.execute_tool_calls_batch(tail, &mut deferred_followups, &mut final_result)
  373                      .await?;
  374              }
  375          } else {
  376              self.execute_tool_calls_batch(tool_calls, &mut deferred_followups, &mut final_result)
  377                  .await?;
  378          }
  379          {
  380              let _span = if !deferred_followups.is_empty() {
  381                  Some(
  382                      tracing::info_span!(
  383                          "tools.deferred_followups",
  384                          count = deferred_followups.len()
  385                      )
  386                      .entered(),
  387                  )
  388              } else {
  389                  None
  390              };
  391              for chat in deferred_followups {
  392                  self.chat_state_handle.push_user_message(chat);
  393              }
  394          }
  395          self.drain_pending_interjections().await;
  396          self.flush_pending_skill_reminders().await;
  397          if let Some(final_result) = final_result {
  398              return Ok(final_result);
  399          }
  400          Ok(ToolLoop::Continue)
  401      }
  402      /// Prepare → dispatch → post-flight. Caller owns the outer tail flush.
  403      async fn execute_tool_calls_batch(
  404          &self,
  405          tool_calls: Vec<crate::sampling::types::ToolCallResponse>,
  406          deferred_followups: &mut Vec<ConversationItem>,
  407          final_result: &mut Option<ToolLoop>,
  408      ) -> Result<(), acp::Error> {
  409          if self.permissions.is_auto_mode() {
  410              let conversation = self.chat_state_handle.get_conversation().await;
  411              super::refresh_classifier_transcript(&self.permissions, &conversation);
  412          }
  413          let mut approved: Vec<PreparedToolCall> = Vec::new();
  414          for call in tool_calls.into_iter() {
  415              if final_result.is_some() {
  416                  let message = match &*final_result {
  417                      Some(ToolLoop::PermissionReject { .. }) => {
  418                          format!(
  419                              "Tool execution cancelled due to earlier permission rejection for tool `{}`",
  420                              call.function.name
  421                          )
  422                      }
  423                      Some(ToolLoop::Cancelled) => {
  424                          format!(
  425                              "Tool execution cancelled due to earlier user cancellation for tool `{}`",
  426                              call.function.name
  427                          )
  428                      }
  429                      Some(ToolLoop::FollowupMessage(_)) => {
  430                          format!(
  431                              "Tool execution cancelled due to earlier user followup message for tool `{}`",
  432                              call.function.name
  433                          )
  434                      }
  435                      _ => {
  436                          format!("Tool execution cancelled for tool `{}`", call.function.name)
  437                      }
  438                  };
  439                  self.chat_state_handle
  440                      .push_tool_result(ConversationItem::tool_result(call.id.clone(), message));
  441                  continue;
  442              }
  443              self.emit_event(crate::session::events::Event::ToolStarted {
  444                  tool_name: call.function.name.clone(),
  445              });
  446              self.observability_bridge
  447                  .emit(
  448                      xai_tool_protocol::session_event::SessionEvent::ToolCallStarted {
  449                          tool_call_id: call.id.clone(),
  450                          tool_name: call.function.name.clone(),
  451                          turn_number: self.current_turn_number.get(),
  452                      },
  453                  )
  454                  .await;
  455              let call_name = call.function.name.clone();
  456              match self.prepare_tool_call(call, deferred_followups).await? {
  457                  Ok(prepared) => approved.push(prepared),
  458                  Err(tool_loop) => {
  459                      self.events.tool_finished();
  460                      if let Some((server, tool)) =
  461                          crate::session::mcp_servers::parse_mcp_tool_name(&call_name)
  462                      {
  463                          let error_reason = match &tool_loop {
  464                              ToolLoop::PermissionReject { reason, .. } => reason.clone(),
  465                              ToolLoop::Cancelled => "cancelled".to_string(),
  466                              ToolLoop::FollowupMessage(_) => "followup".to_string(),
  467                              ToolLoop::HookDenied { hook_name, .. } => {
  468                                  format!("hook_denied:{hook_name}")
  469                              }
  470                              other => format!("{other:?}"),
  471                          };
  472                          self.emit_event(xai_grok_session_events::Event::McpToolCallCompleted {
  473                              server_name: server.to_string(),
  474                              tool_name: tool.to_string(),
  475                              call_id: format!(
  476                                  "{}{}{}",
  477                                  server,
  478                                  crate::session::mcp_servers::MCP_TOOL_NAME_DELIMITER,
  479                                  tool
  480                              ),
  481                              duration_ms: 0,
  482                              success: false,
  483                              is_timeout: false,
  484                              error: Some(error_reason),
  485                              reconnect_attempted: false,
  486                              auth_retry_attempted: false,
  487                          });
  488                      }
  489                      if matches!(
  490                          tool_loop,
  491                          ToolLoop::PermissionReject { .. }
  492                              | ToolLoop::Cancelled
  493                              | ToolLoop::FollowupMessage(_)
  494                      ) && final_result.is_none()
  495                      {
  496                          *final_result = Some(tool_loop);
  497                      }
  498                  }
  499              }
  500          }
  501          if approved.iter().any(|p| p.tool_name == "search_tool") {
  502              self.retry_auth_required_servers().await;
  503          }
  504          let write_paths: std::collections::HashSet<String> = approved
  505              .iter()
  506              .filter(|prepared| !prepared.is_read_only)
  507              .filter_map(|prepared| lock_path_for_args(&prepared.parsed_args).map(str::to_owned))
  508              .collect();
  509          let file_locks = {
  510              let mut map: std::collections::HashMap<String, Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<()>>> =
  511                  std::collections::HashMap::new();
  512              for prepared in &approved {
  513                  if let Some(fp) = lock_path_for_args(&prepared.parsed_args)
  514                      && write_paths.contains(fp)
  515                  {
  516                      map.entry(fp.to_owned())
  517                          .or_insert_with(|| Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(())));
  518                  }
  519              }
  520              map
02
L1facttools

02 · 工具参数对模型瑕疵有恢复层

源码事实

prepare_tool_call 会规范空参数、尝试解析 JSON；遇到拼接的多个 JSON 对象会逐个试配工具 schema，选出匹配对象；仍无法解析才产生 ToolParsingError。

白话解释

模型偶尔把两个 JSON 粘在一起，Grok Build 会先抢救，不是一看到格式错就整轮失败。

对自研 Harness 的含义

对模型输出做保守修复能提高成功率，但必须记录恢复行为，避免悄悄改变语义。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 878–913
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 914–950
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

 1020              self.handle_tool_not_executed(&call.id, &tool_call_id, msg)
 1021                  .await?;
06
DIMENSION · CONNECTORS

连接器、插件与 MCP

1 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L2factconnectors

01 · 一个插件可同时交付 Skills、Commands、Agents、Hooks、MCP、LSP

源码事实

PluginManifest 明确定义六类组件；所有路径必须位于 canonical plugin root。PluginRegistry 只把 enabled 且 trusted 的插件作为 active，项目插件需要显式信任，disabled 冲突优先。

白话解释

插件不是只加一个工具，而是可以连同说明书、快捷命令、子 Agent 角色、策略 hook、远程工具和语言服务一起打包。

对自研 Harness 的含义

生态能力完整，但插件供应链和信任 UI 变成核心安全面。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 103–135
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 136–170
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

DIMENSION · SECURITY

安全、审批与权限

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factsecurity

01 · Plan Mode 的只读约束独立于 Always Approve

源码事实

plan_mode_edit_gate 在权限管理器之前执行，即使 YOLO/always-approve 也不能绕过；普通编辑只允许计划文件，apply_patch 因无法预知目标而保守拒绝，未知退出结果默认取消。

白话解释

“全部自动批准”也不等于“计划阶段可以乱改代码”。计划模式另有一把更早、更硬的锁。

对自研 Harness 的含义

工作流阶段约束不能依赖通用权限模式，否则高权限模式会破坏阶段不变量。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 157–180
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 181–205
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

crates/codegen/xai-grok-shell/src/session/acp_session_impl/tool_calls.rs:157–205固定提交 ↗
  157  /// Verdict for a tool call evaluated against the plan-mode edit gate.
  158  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  159  pub(super) enum PlanEditGate {
  160      /// Execute normally (plan mode inactive, not an edit, or allowed target).
  161      Allow,
  162      /// Grok-toolset edit outside the plan file (plan-file-only rule).
  163      RejectNonPlanFile,
  164  }
  165  /// Gate edit-class tool calls while plan mode is active.
  166  ///
  167  /// Plan mode is read-only **in every permission mode, including
  168  /// always-approve**: the permission manager's YOLO fast path deliberately
  169  /// knows nothing about plan mode, so this gate — not the permission system —
  170  /// is what enforces it. Two rules, matching the two toolsets' contracts:
  171  ///
  172  /// - **Compat-toolset `Write`/`StrReplace`**: any markdown
  173  ///   file is editable in plan mode (plan docs are written with these
  174  ///   same tools); everything else is rejected. Pre-existing behavior.
  175  /// - **Compat-toolset `Delete`** is **not** on the markdown carve-out: it maps to
  176  ///   `AccessKind::Edit` and is plan-file-only (same as grok edits). Deleting
  177  ///   an arbitrary `.md` in plan mode must not pass.
  178  /// - **Every other edit tool** (`AccessKind::Edit`) is restricted to the plan
  179  ///   file itself, via the same predicate that auto-approves plan-file edits
  180  ///   ([`PlanModeTracker::should_auto_approve_edit`]) so the gate and the
  181  ///   permission bypass can never disagree.
  182  ///
  183  /// `apply_patch` maps to a placeholder `AccessKind::Edit("apply_patch")` and
  184  /// therefore never matches the plan file: it is always rejected in plan mode
  185  /// (conservative — per-file targets are only known after patch parsing).
  186  /// Non-edit tools (bash, read, grep, MCP, web) are never gated here; they
  187  /// flow to the normal permission path, where yolo may still auto-approve
  188  /// them. `enter_plan_mode` / `exit_plan_mode` map to `AccessKind::Read` and
  189  /// are likewise never gated.
  190  pub(super) fn plan_mode_edit_gate(
  191      tracker: &crate::session::plan_mode::PlanModeTracker,
  192      tool_input: &ToolInput,
  193      access_kind: &AccessKind,
  194  ) -> PlanEditGate {
  195      if !tracker.is_active() {
  196          return PlanEditGate::Allow;
  197      }
  198      let _ = tool_input;
  199      match access_kind {
  200          AccessKind::Edit(path) if !tracker.should_auto_approve_edit(Path::new(path)) => {
  201              PlanEditGate::RejectNonPlanFile
  202          }
  203          _ => PlanEditGate::Allow,
  204      }
  205  }
  206  /// Typed view of an `exit_plan_mode` approval decision. The wire type
  207  /// (`ExitPlanModeExtResponse`) carries `outcome` as a string; both the mid-turn
  208  /// intercept and the resume re-park match on this enum instead. Unknown /
  209  /// unrecognized outcomes map to [`Cancelled`](Self::Cancelled) so the session
  210  /// fails CLOSED (stays in plan mode) rather than auto-approving.
  211  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  212  pub(super) enum PlanApprovalOutcome {
  213      Approved,
  214      Cancelled,
  215      Abandoned,
  216  }
  217  impl PlanApprovalOutcome {

  220      ) -> Self {
  221          match resp.outcome.as_str() {
  222              "approved" => Self::Approved,
  223              "abandoned" => Self::Abandoned,
  224              _ => Self::Cancelled,
  225          }
  226      }
  227  }
  228  /// Classify an `ext_method` failure: `true` when the reverse-request could not
  229  /// be DELIVERED to any client (no interactive client wired — headless / SDK),
  230  /// `false` when it was delivered but the client went away before answering
  231  /// (quit / disconnect / leader restart).
  232  ///
  233  /// Uses `xai_acp_lib`'s TYPED [`AcpChannelFailure`](xai_acp_lib::AcpChannelFailure)
  234  /// discriminant (carried in the error's `data`) rather than substring-matching
  235  /// another crate's message text: `SendFailed` (enqueue failed → no connection) →
  236  /// `true`; `RecvFailed` (delivered then dropped) → `false`. Any other error
  237  /// (including a non-`acp_send` error) defaults to `false` so the approval is
  238  /// kept pending and never auto-approved.
  239  fn ext_method_no_client(err: &acp::Error) -> bool {
  240      matches!(
  241          xai_acp_lib::acp_channel_failure(err),
  242          Some(xai_acp_lib::AcpChannelFailure::SendFailed)
  243      )
  244  }

  161  /// Whether to intercept exit-plan tools for client-side plan approval.
  162  ///
  163  /// A mode-switch back to agent with `PlanFileRead::Absent` skips intercept
  164  /// (leaving without approving is allowed). `Present` / `Unreadable` still intercept
  165  /// (unreadable = fail-closed, empty approval UI rather than silent exit).
  166  pub(super) fn should_intercept_exit_plan_approval(
  167      is_exit_plan_mode: bool,
  168      is_cursor_switch_to_agent: bool,
  169      is_cursor_create_plan: bool,
  170      plan_read: &PlanFileRead,
  171  ) -> bool {
  172      if !is_exit_plan_mode && !is_cursor_switch_to_agent && !is_cursor_create_plan {
  173          return false;
  174      }
  175      if is_cursor_switch_to_agent && matches!(plan_read, PlanFileRead::Absent) {
  176          return false;
  177      }
  178      true
  179  }

  183          return true;
  184      }
  185      false
  186  }

  202          .partition(|call| !is_file_backed_exit_plan_kind(kind_of(&call.function.name)))
  203  }
  204  /// Verdict for a tool call evaluated against the plan-mode edit gate.
  205  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  206  pub(super) enum PlanEditGate {
  207      /// Execute normally (plan mode inactive, not an edit, or allowed target).
  208      Allow,
  209      /// Grok-toolset edit outside the plan file (plan-file-only rule).
  210      RejectNonPlanFile,
  211  }
  212  /// Gate edit-class tool calls while plan mode is active.
  213  ///
  214  /// Plan mode is read-only **in every permission mode, including
  215  /// always-approve**: the permission manager's YOLO fast path deliberately
  216  /// knows nothing about plan mode, so this gate — not the permission system —
  217  /// is what enforces it. Two rules, matching the two toolsets' contracts:
  218  ///
  219  /// - **Compat-toolset `Write`/`StrReplace`**: any markdown
  220  ///   file is editable in plan mode (plan docs are written with these
  221  ///   same tools); everything else is rejected. Pre-existing behavior.
  222  /// - **Compat-toolset `Delete`** is **not** on the markdown carve-out: it maps to
  223  ///   `AccessKind::Edit` and is plan-file-only (same as grok edits). Deleting
  224  ///   an arbitrary `.md` in plan mode must not pass.
  225  /// - **Every other edit tool** (`AccessKind::Edit`) is restricted to the plan
  226  ///   file itself, via the same predicate that auto-approves plan-file edits
  227  ///   ([`PlanModeTracker::should_auto_approve_edit`]) so the gate and the
  228  ///   permission bypass can never disagree.
  229  ///
  230  /// `apply_patch` maps to a placeholder `AccessKind::Edit("apply_patch")` and
  231  /// therefore never matches the plan file: it is always rejected in plan mode
  232  /// (conservative — per-file targets are only known after patch parsing).
  233  /// Non-edit tools (bash, read, grep, MCP, web) are never gated here; they
  234  /// flow to the normal permission path, where yolo may still auto-approve
  235  /// them. `enter_plan_mode` / `exit_plan_mode` map to `AccessKind::Read` and
  236  /// are likewise never gated.
  237  pub(super) fn plan_mode_edit_gate(
  238      tracker: &crate::session::plan_mode::PlanModeTracker,
  239      tool_input: &ToolInput,
  240      access_kind: &AccessKind,
  241  ) -> PlanEditGate {
  242      if !tracker.is_active() {
  243          return PlanEditGate::Allow;
  244      }
  245      let _ = tool_input;
  246      match access_kind {
  247          AccessKind::Edit(path) if !tracker.should_auto_approve_edit(Path::new(path)) => {
  248              PlanEditGate::RejectNonPlanFile
  249          }
  250          _ => PlanEditGate::Allow,
  251      }
  252  }
  253  /// Typed view of an `exit_plan_mode` approval decision. The wire type
  254  /// (`ExitPlanModeExtResponse`) carries `outcome` as a string; both the mid-turn
  255  /// intercept and the resume re-park match on this enum instead. Unknown /
  256  /// unrecognized outcomes map to [`Cancelled`](Self::Cancelled) so the session
  257  /// fails CLOSED (stays in plan mode) rather than auto-approving.
  258  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
  259  pub(super) enum PlanApprovalOutcome {
  260      Approved,
  261      Cancelled,
  262      Abandoned,
  263  }
  264  impl PlanApprovalOutcome {
  265      fn from_response(
  266          resp: &xai_grok_tools::implementations::grok_build::exit_plan_mode::ExitPlanModeExtResponse,
  267      ) -> Self {
  268          match resp.outcome.as_str() {
  269              "approved" => Self::Approved,
  270              "abandoned" => Self::Abandoned,
  271              _ => Self::Cancelled,
  272          }
  273      }
  274  }
  275  /// Classify an `ext_method` failure: `true` when the reverse-request could not
  276  /// be DELIVERED to any client (no interactive client wired — headless / SDK),
02
L1factsecurity

02 · 权限判断理解访问类型和会话上下文

源码事实

工具被分类为 Read、Edit、Bash、Grep、MCP、Web；Ask/Auto/Always Approve 三种模式分别走交互、带最近对话的分类器或快速放行，决定来源与等待时间进入 trace 和 telemetry。

白话解释

它不是只看工具名，而是知道“这是读哪个路径、改哪个文件、跑什么命令、访问哪个网站”，自动模式还会参考最近几轮对话。

对自研 Harness 的含义

权限系统既是策略引擎，也是可观测的决策服务。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1035–1067
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1068–1102
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

L1factsecurity

03 · PreToolUse Hook 可在权限前阻断

源码事实

工具解析和 Plan gate 后，运行服务端/客户端 PreToolUse hooks；任一 hook 的 Deny 会产生 HookDenied，不再进入权限请求和 dispatch。

白话解释

企业策略脚本可以比用户批准更早说“不”，避免用户误点同意覆盖组织规则。

对自研 Harness 的含义

Hook 是治理层而不仅是通知回调。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 977–1004
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1005–1034
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

08
DIMENSION · OBSERVABILITY

观测、日志与回放

1 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factobservability

01 · 工具、权限、压缩和沙箱均产出结构化事件

源码事实

工具开始/完成进入 ACP 与统一 SessionEvent，权限记录模式、来源、等待时间，压缩 span 记录 token/尝试/拒绝/TTFT，沙箱事件立即追加 ~/.grok/sandbox-events.jsonl。

白话解释

不仅能看到“失败了”，还能回答失败在哪个关、谁批准的、等了多久、压缩试了几次、沙箱挡了什么。

对自研 Harness 的含义

具备平台级诊断基础，但事件面很大，需要统一 trace/session/tool/subagent ID 规范。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 392–413
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 414–436
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  402      /// Prepare → dispatch → post-flight. Caller owns the outer tail flush.
  403      async fn execute_tool_calls_batch(
  404          &self,
  405          tool_calls: Vec<crate::sampling::types::ToolCallResponse>,
  406          deferred_followups: &mut Vec<ConversationItem>,
  407          final_result: &mut Option<ToolLoop>,
  408      ) -> Result<(), acp::Error> {
  409          if self.permissions.is_auto_mode() {
  410              let conversation = self.chat_state_handle.get_conversation().await;
  411              super::refresh_classifier_transcript(&self.permissions, &conversation);
  412          }
  413          let mut approved: Vec<PreparedToolCall> = Vec::new();
  414          for call in tool_calls.into_iter() {
  415              if final_result.is_some() {
  416                  let message = match &*final_result {
  417                      Some(ToolLoop::PermissionReject { .. }) => {
  418                          format!(
  419                              "Tool execution cancelled due to earlier permission rejection for tool `{}`",
  420                              call.function.name
  421                          )
  422                      }
  423                      Some(ToolLoop::Cancelled) => {
  424                          format!(
  425                              "Tool execution cancelled due to earlier user cancellation for tool `{}`",
  426                              call.function.name
  427                          )
  428                      }
  429                      Some(ToolLoop::FollowupMessage(_)) => {
  430                          format!(
  431                              "Tool execution cancelled due to earlier user followup message for tool `{}`",
  432                              call.function.name
  433                          )
  434                      }
  435                      _ => {
  436                          format!("Tool execution cancelled for tool `{}`", call.function.name)
  437                      }
  438                  };
  439                  self.chat_state_handle
  440                      .push_tool_result(ConversationItem::tool_result(call.id.clone(), message));
  441                  continue;
  442              }
  443              self.emit_event(crate::session::events::Event::ToolStarted {
  444                  tool_name: call.function.name.clone(),
  445              });
  446              self.observability_bridge
  447                  .emit(
  448                      xai_tool_protocol::session_event::SessionEvent::ToolCallStarted {
  449                          tool_call_id: call.id.clone(),
  450                          tool_name: call.function.name.clone(),
  451                          turn_number: self.current_turn_number.get(),
  452                      },
  453                  )
  454                  .await;
  455              let call_name = call.function.name.clone();
  456              match self.prepare_tool_call(call, deferred_followups).await? {
  457                  Ok(prepared) => approved.push(prepared),
  458                  Err(tool_loop) => {
  459                      self.events.tool_finished();
  460                      if let Some((server, tool)) =
  461                          crate::session::mcp_servers::parse_mcp_tool_name(&call_name)
  462                      {
  463                          let error_reason = match &tool_loop {
  464                              ToolLoop::PermissionReject { reason, .. } => reason.clone(),
  465                              ToolLoop::Cancelled => "cancelled".to_string(),
  466                              ToolLoop::FollowupMessage(_) => "followup".to_string(),
  467                              ToolLoop::HookDenied { hook_name, .. } => {
  468                                  format!("hook_denied:{hook_name}")
  469                              }
  470                              other => format!("{other:?}"),
  471                          };
  472                          self.emit_event(xai_grok_session_events::Event::McpToolCallCompleted {
  473                              server_name: server.to_string(),
  474                              tool_name: tool.to_string(),
  475                              call_id: format!(
  476                                  "{}{}{}",
  477                                  server,
  478                                  crate::session::mcp_servers::MCP_TOOL_NAME_DELIMITER,
  479                                  tool
  480                              ),
  481                              duration_ms: 0,
  482                              success: false,
  483                              is_timeout: false,
  484                              error: Some(error_reason),
  485                              reconnect_attempted: false,
  486                              auth_retry_attempted: false,
  487                          });
  488                      }
  489                      if matches!(
  490                          tool_loop,
  491                          ToolLoop::PermissionReject { .. }
  492                              | ToolLoop::Cancelled
  493                              | ToolLoop::FollowupMessage(_)
  494                      ) && final_result.is_none()
  495                      {
  496                          *final_result = Some(tool_loop);
  497                      }
  498                  }
  499              }
  500          }
  501          if approved.iter().any(|p| p.tool_name == "search_tool") {
  502              self.retry_auth_required_servers().await;
  503          }
  504          let write_paths: std::collections::HashSet<String> = approved
  505              .iter()
  506              .filter(|prepared| !prepared.is_read_only)
  507              .filter_map(|prepared| lock_path_for_args(&prepared.parsed_args).map(str::to_owned))
09
DIMENSION · COLLABORATION

协作与子 Agent

1 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factcollaboration

01 · 子 Agent 支持后台执行、恢复、深度限制与 worktree 隔离

源码事实

task/spawn_subagent 默认后台运行；启动前校验类型白名单和 toggle，能力取父子交集；可选创建独立 Git worktree，持久化子会话并从 snapshot 恢复；达到 max depth 后移除子 Agent 的 task 工具。

白话解释

它是真正的多 Agent 调度：子任务可以后台跑、以后接着跑、在独立分支目录里改代码，但不能无限生孩子。

对自研 Harness 的含义

并行协作能力强，同时引入 worktree 生命周期、结果合并、成本与递归治理的高复杂度。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 811–824
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 825–839
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

DIMENSION · INSTRUCTIONS

全部指令与扩展面

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factinstructions

01 · 项目指令跨 Grok、Claude、Cursor 生态发现

源码事实

从 home、仓库根到 cwd 分层发现 AGENTS/Claude 指令与 .grok/.claude/.cursor rules，按兼容开关和 gitignore 过滤、canonical path 去重；更深目录排在后面。

白话解释

它能读懂多个编辑器生态留下的项目规则，并按“越靠近当前目录越具体”叠加。

对自研 Harness 的含义

兼容性高，但必须在报告中展示来源和优先级，避免用户不知道哪条规则生效。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–3
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 4–7
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

crates/codegen/xai-grok-agent/src/prompt/agents_md.rs:1–7固定提交 ↗
    1  //! AGENTS.md / Claude.md / rules directory discovery and loading.
    2  //!
    3  //! Searches from cwd to repo root, plus `~/.grok/`. Also discovers
    4  //! `*.md` files in rules directories: vendor-prefixed `.grok/rules/`,
    5  //! `.claude/rules/`, and `.cursor/rules/` in project directories, and a
    6  //! plain `rules/` directly under the vendor-qualified home-scope roots
    7  //! (`~/.grok/rules/`, `~/.claude/rules/`, `~/.cursor/rules/`).
    8
    9  use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
   10
   11  use crate::prompt::ignore::{build_gitignore, is_ignored};
   12
   13  use xai_grok_tools::types::compat::CompatConfig;
   14
   15  /// Represents an agent config file with its path and content.
   16  #[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
   17  pub struct AgentConfigFile {
   18      /// The filename (e.g., "AGENTS.md", "Claude.md")
   19      pub file_name: String,
   20      /// The full absolute path to the config file
   21      pub file_path: String,
   22      /// The content of the config file
   23      pub content: String,
   24  }
   25
   26  /// Find matching agent config files in a directory.
   27  ///
   28  /// `filenames` is the (compat-gated) recognized list, precomputed once by the
   29  /// caller so the cwd→root walk doesn't re-allocate it per directory. When all
   30  /// cells are on it equals the legacy `AGENT_FILENAMES` list exactly.
   31  fn find_agent_files(dir: &Path, filenames: &[&str]) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
   32      filenames
   33          .iter()
   34          .filter_map(|name| {
   35              let path = dir.join(name);
   36              path.exists().then_some(path)
   37          })
   38          .collect()
   39  }
   40
   41  /// Find `*.md` files in `.grok/rules/`, `.claude/rules/`, and `.cursor/rules/`,
   42  /// sorted alphabetically. `rules_subdirs` is the (compat-gated) list, precomputed
   43  /// once by the caller so the walk doesn't re-allocate it per directory.
   44  fn find_rules_files(dir: &Path, rules_subdirs: &[&str]) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
   45      let mut results = Vec::new();
   46      for rules_subdir in rules_subdirs {
   47          let rules_dir = dir.join(rules_subdir);
   48          if !rules_dir.is_dir() {
   49              continue;
   50          }
   51          let mut entries: Vec<PathBuf> = match std::fs::read_dir(&rules_dir) {
   52              Ok(iter) => iter
   53                  .filter_map(|entry| entry.ok())
   54                  .map(|e| e.path())
   55                  .filter(|p| {
   56                      p.extension()
   57                          .and_then(|ext| ext.to_str())
   58                          .is_some_and(|ext| ext.eq_ignore_ascii_case("md"))
   59                  })
   60                  .collect(),
   61              Err(_) => continue,
   62          };
   63          entries.sort_by(|a, b| a.file_name().cmp(&b.file_name()));
   64          results.extend(entries);
   65      }
   66      results
   67  }
   68
   69  /// Canonicalize a path for discovery deduplication, falling back to the
   70  /// original path when canonicalization fails.
   71  fn canonical_for_dedup(path: &Path) -> PathBuf {
   72      dunce::canonicalize(path).unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_path_buf())
   73  }
   74
   75  struct DiscoveryRoot {
   76      path: PathBuf,
   77      canonical_path: PathBuf,
   78      scan_named_files: bool,

02
L2factinstructions

02 · PromptContext 是可序列化、可检查的一等契约

源码事实

PromptContext 保存版本、模式、受众、模板、指令文件、memory、角色、OS/shell/cwd/date 与交互模式；Primary/Subagent 选择不同基础模板，但子 Agent 仍拿到完整 AGENTS.md。

白话解释

系统提示词的输入不是散落变量，而是一张可以导出检查的配置表。

对自研 Harness 的含义

Prompt provenance 和可重放性明显优于只在运行时拼字符串。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 80–115
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 116–152
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。
