crates/codegen/xai-grok-agent/src/prompt/context.rs:80–152固定提交 ↗
   80  /// Agent-specific inputs for system prompt rendering.
   81  ///
   82  /// Serializable (JSON/YAML) so users can dump it and inspect fields.
   83  /// Rendering goes through `ToolBridge::render_prompt()`.
   84  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   85  pub struct PromptContext {
   86      /// Schema version for forward-compatible persistence.
   87      pub version: u32,
   88      /// Which prompt mode produced this context.
   89      pub prompt_mode: PromptMode,
   90      /// Whether this is a primary (parent) or subagent (child) session.
   91      /// Controls base template choice and catalog section rendering.
   92      #[serde(default)]
   93      pub audience: PromptAudience,
   94      /// Custom body: appended after base template (Extend) or the entire
   95      /// prompt (Full). `None` = base template only.
   96      #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
   97      pub prompt_body: Option<String>,
   98      /// Which base template to use for `Extend` mode.
   99      /// `TemplateOverride::None` = standard base/subagent template.
  100      /// `TemplateOverride::Codex` = apply-patch profile template (decrypted on demand).
  101      /// `TemplateOverride::Custom` = caller-provided template string.
  102      #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_template_override_none")]
  103      pub system_prompt: TemplateOverride,
  104      /// AGENTS.md files discovered during build, in precedence order
  105      /// (repo root → CWD; deeper files override).
  106      pub agents_md_files: Vec<AgentConfigFile>,
  107      /// Pre-rendered persona summaries for system prompt injection.
  108      /// Each entry is a formatted string like:
  109      /// `- **reviewer** [user]: Writes structured review notes...`
  110      #[serde(default)]
  111      pub persona_summaries: Vec<String>,
  112      /// ISO-8601 UTC timestamp captured at build time.
  113      pub build_timestamp_utc: String,
  114      /// Whether the memory system is enabled for this session.
  115      /// When true, the system prompt includes a `<memory>` section telling
  116      /// the model it can use `memory_search` and `memory_get`.
  117      #[serde(default)]
  118      pub memory_enabled: bool,
  119      #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  120      pub memory_global_path: Option<String>,
  121      #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  122      pub memory_workspace_path: Option<String>,
  123      /// Role instructions to include in the system prompt.
  124      /// Moved from the user task prompt so they're part of durable identity.
  125      #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  126      pub role_instructions: Option<String>,
  127      /// Persona instructions to include in the system prompt.
  128      /// Moved from the user task prompt so they're part of durable identity.
  129      #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  130      pub persona_instructions: Option<String>,
  131      /// OS name for the `<user_info>` system prompt block.
  132      #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  133      pub os_name: Option<String>,
  134      /// User's default shell for the `<user_info>` system prompt block.
  135      #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  136      pub shell_path: Option<String>,
  137      /// Model-facing working directory for the `<user_info>` system prompt block.
  138      #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  139      pub working_directory: Option<String>,
  140      /// Current date (`YYYY-MM-DD`) in the user's local timezone, for the
  141      /// `<user_info>` system prompt block.
  142      #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
  143      pub current_date: Option<String>,
  144      /// Whether the agent is running in a non-interactive (headless / SDK /
  145      /// stdio / generic-ACP).
  146      #[serde(default)]
  147      pub is_non_interactive: bool,
  148      /// Identity in the primary grok-build system prompt (`You are <label>…`).
  149      /// Not the UI picker name. Defaults to [`DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT_LABEL`].
  150      #[serde(default = "default_system_prompt_label")]
  151      pub system_prompt_label: String,
  152  }
  153  /// Default identity on trim-tool-descriptions (`You are Grok released by xAI`).
  154  pub const DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT_LABEL: &str = "Grok";
  155  fn default_system_prompt_label() -> String {
  156      DEFAULT_SYSTEM_PROMPT_LABEL.to_string()
  157  }
  158  fn is_template_override_none(t: &TemplateOverride) -> bool {
  159      matches!(t, TemplateOverride::None)
  160  }
  161  impl PromptContext {
  162      /// Normalize this context for persistence based on audience.
  163      ///
  164      /// For `Subagent` audience, applies the same suppression as the render
  165      /// path: persona summaries are cleared. AGENTS.md is delivered in full,
  166      /// identical to the primary agent.
  167      pub fn normalize_for_persistence(&mut self) {
  168          if self.audience != PromptAudience::Subagent {
  169              return;
  170          }
  171          self.persona_summaries.clear();
  172      }
  173  }
  174  impl Default for PromptContext {
  175      fn default() -> Self {
  176          Self {
  177              version: 1,
  178              prompt_mode: PromptMode::Extend,
  179              audience: PromptAudience::default(),
  180              prompt_body: None,
  181              system_prompt: TemplateOverride::None,
  182              agents_md_files: vec![],
  183              persona_summaries: vec![],
  184              build_timestamp_utc: chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339(),
  185              memory_enabled: false,
  186              memory_global_path: None,
  187              memory_workspace_path: None,
  188              role_instructions: None,
  189              persona_instructions: None,
  190              os_name: None,
  191              shell_path: None,
  192              working_directory: None,
  193              current_date: None,
  194              is_non_interactive: false,
  195              system_prompt_label: default_system_prompt_label(),
  196          }
  197      }
  198  }
  199  impl PromptContext {
  200      /// Format the AGENTS.md section as a `<system-reminder>` block.
  201      ///
  202      /// Returns `None` if no AGENTS.md files were discovered.
  203      pub fn format_agents_md_section(&self) -> Option<String> {
  204          agents_md::format_agents_md_section(&self.agents_md_files)
  205      }
  206      /// AGENTS.md content for injection as a prepended user message.
  207      ///
  208      /// - Subagents and primary sessions both get the full block, so a child
  209      ///   verifier sees the same project instructions as the main agent.
  210      pub fn agents_md_user_reminder(&self) -> Option<String> {
  211          self.format_agents_md_section()
  212      }
  213      /// Personas content for injection as a prepended user message.
  214      ///
  215      /// Returns the `<system-reminder>` block to prepend as a user message,
  216      /// wrapping the `<personas>` section.
  217      ///
  218      /// - Subagents never get personas (`task` itself is a parent-only tool).
  219      pub fn personas_user_reminder(&self) -> Option<String> {
  220          if self.audience == PromptAudience::Subagent {
  221              return None;
  222          }
  223          let section = self.format_personas_section()?;
